Squash Armor is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Armor]] set that grants a chance to obtain a [[Fermento]]. It requires [[Farming|Farming XXXV (35)]] to use.
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|1x Cropie Helmet ([[Cropie Armor]]) 8x [[Polished Pumpkin]] 20x [[Squash]] 48x [[Enchanted Melon]]|Recipe: [[Squash]] x10 ×2 + [[Polished Pumpkin]] x8 + [[Enchanted Melon]] x24 ×2 + [[Cropie Armor]] → [[Squash Armor]]|
|None|1x Cropie Chestplate ([[Cropie Armor]]) 8x [[Polished Pumpkin]] 20x [[Squash]] 30x [[Enchanted Cookie]] 48x [[Enchanted Melon]]|Recipe: [[Squash]] x10 ×2 + [[Cropie Armor]] + [[Enchanted Melon]] x24 ×2 + [[Polished Pumpkin]] x8 + [[Enchanted Cookie]] x10 ×3 → [[Squash Armor]]|
|None|1x Cropie Leggings ([[Cropie Armor]]) 8x [[Polished Pumpkin]] 20x [[Enchanted Cookie]] 20x [[Squash]] 48x [[Enchanted Melon]]|Recipe: [[Squash]] x10 ×2 + [[Polished Pumpkin]] x8 + [[Enchanted Melon]] x24 ×2 + [[Cropie Armor]] + [[Enchanted Cookie]] x10 ×2 → [[Squash Armor]]|
|None|1x Cropie Boots ([[Cropie Armor]]) 12x [[Polished Pumpkin]] 20x [[Squash]]|Recipe: [[Squash]] x10 ×2 + [[Polished Pumpkin]] x6 ×2 + [[Cropie Armor]] → [[Squash Armor]]|

While wearing Squash Armor and collecting crops, there is a chance to harvest a [[Fermento]] when breaking [[Sugar Cane]], [[Cactus]], [[Mushroom]], and [[Nether Wart]].

# Stability Elixir
Categories: Rare items, Items
[Stability Elixir]
Type: Item
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✕ No
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Rift Transferrable ([[Rift Dimension]]): ✕ No
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: RIFT_STABILITY_ELIXIR
Tooltip: Useful to stay integral in case you've accidentally inverted yourself.; RARE
The Stability Elixir is a [[Rarity|RARE]] item.
## Obtaining
The Stability Elixir can be obtained from the [[Argofay Serialbather]] after speaking to [[Inverted Sirius]] and a variety of [[NPC|NPCs]] in the [[Shifted Tavern]].
## Usage
The player can bring a Stability Elixir to [[Inverted Sirius]] in order to obtain access to the [[Rift Dimension|Rift Transfer Chest]].
## Trivia
- Despite allegedly costing 500 [[Motes]] in [[Shifted]]'s UI, it only costs 1x [[Shy Crux]].
- The player must spend a total of 160 [[Motes]] on tips before [[Fafnir]] reveals who the supplier is.
- The Stability Elixir is the only elixir that does not grant any effects and is not purchased from [[Shifted]].
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|May 4, 2023|Added Stability Elixir.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Jun 20, 2023|0.19|Added Stability Elixir.|

The Staff of the Volcano is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Wands|Wand]] that creates an explosion, dealing a large amount of damage and setting all nearby mobs on fire.

The Stalagmight Shard is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Atomized Glacite]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Fusion
A Stalagmight Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]] and [[Attribute Fusion|Special Fusion]]. For [[Attribute Fusion|Special Fusion]], the following input conditions must be met:
- First: [[Quartzfang Shard]]
- Second: [[Silentdepth Shard]] or Cave Dweller Family
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Stalagmight Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Atomized Glacite]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Atomized Glacite ([[Attributes]]); Gain +1%–10% extra ᠅ [[Glacite Powder]].
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].

|Ⅱ (2)|2|3|+2 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅲ (3)|3|6|+3 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅳ (4)|3|9|+4 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅴ (5)|4|13|+5 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅵ (6)|4|17|+6 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅶ (7)|5|22|+7 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅷ (8)|6|28|+8 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅸ (9)|8|36|+9 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅹ (10)|12|48|+10 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|

A Stalagmight Shard can be used in [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]] to create other [[Attributes|Shards]].

## Lore
When viewed in the [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] or [[Bazaar]], the Stalagmight Shard has the following description:
- Its presence chills the air around it, turning the cave into a frozen tomb as it relentlessly pursues intruders.

### Hunting
A Star Sentry Shard can be obtained from [[Hunting]] a [[Star Sentry]] with a [[Black Holes|Black Hole]] or enough [[Charm Chance]] to [[Charm]] it.
### Fusion
A Star Sentry Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|ID Fusion]] and [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]].

# Starfall
Categories: Rare items, Items in Sacks, Items, Mob Drops, Crafting ingredients, Dwarven Mines, Material
[Starfall]
Type: Item
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
Tooltip Text: No one knows how and when the Starfall appeared. All we know is that it was already there when the Dwarves discovered the cavern.

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: STARFALL
Tooltip: No one knows how and when the Starfall appeared. All we know is that it was already there when the Dwarves discovered the cavern.; RARE
Starfall is a [[Rarity|RARE]] material used to craft a [[Beacon]] and fuel it through the use of a [[Power Crystal]]. Starfall is also used to make the [[Petrified Starfall]] [[Reforge Stones|Reforge Stone]].
## Obtaining
### Mob Drops
|Level|Mob|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[Lv61]|[[Treasure Hoarder]]|1|6Rare Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
|[Lv61]|[[Treasure Hoarder]]|1|6Rare Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|50%|
|[Lv135]|[[Star Sentry]]|1|4Uncommon Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|20%|
The Starfall dropped from [[Star Sentry|Star Sentries]] drop per hit, instead of on kill.
### Fallen Stars
Mining within range of a [[Fallen Stars|Fallen Star]] has a 2% chance to drop [[Starfall]] 1 Starfall.
### Cult of the Fallen Star
[[Starfall]] 200 (3 stacks plus 8) Starfall can be repeatedly obtained by speaking to the [[Cult of the Fallen Star]].
## Usage
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|Beacon I ([[Beacon]])|Mithril IX (9) ([[Mithril]])|5x [[Glass]] [[Starfall]] 64x Starfall 192x (3 stacks (192 items total)) [[Enchanted Mithril]]|Recipe: [[Glass]] ×5 + Starfall x64 + [[Enchanted Mithril]] x64 ×3 → [[Beacon]]|
|[[Meteor Chunk]]|Ghast Tear VI (6) ([[Ghast Tear]])|10x [[Tentacle Meat]] [[Starfall]] 20x Starfall 40x [[Silver Fang]]|Recipe: [[Silver Fang]] x10 ×4 + Starfall x5 ×4 + [[Tentacle Meat]] x10 → [[Meteor Chunk]]|
### Forging

|-|-|-|-|
|[[Power Crystal]]|[[Power Crystal]]|HotM II (2) ([[Heart of the Mountain]])|1 [[Power Crystal]] [[Starfall]] 256 (4 stacks) Starfall|
|[[Starfall Seasoning]]|[[Starfall Seasoning]]|HotM II (2) ([[Heart of the Mountain]])|1 [[Starfall Seasoning]] 16 [[Treasurite]] [[Starfall]] 64 (1 stack) Starfall|
|[[Petrified Starfall]]|[[Petrified Starfall]]|HotM III (3) ([[Heart of the Mountain]])|1 [[Petrified Starfall]] [[Starfall]] 512 (8 stacks) Starfall|

# Starfall Seasoning
Categories: Rare items, Items, Tools, Mining, Dwarven Mines, Forged items
[Starfall Seasoning]
Type: Drill Part ([[Drill Parts]])
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
HotM ([[Heart of the Mountain]]) Requirement: Heart of the Mountain II (2) ([[Heart of the Mountain]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✔ Yes (+3 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]]))
[Materials]
Raw Materials (All materials in their most basic form needed to obtain the item.): 64x [[Starfall]] 16x [[Treasurite]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: STARFALL_SEASONING
Tooltip: Drill Part; Grants +25 Mining Speed and +10 Mining Fortune. Doubled when within 20 blocks of a Fallen Star.; Put this item in the Upgrade Module slot of a Drill at a Drill Mechanic!; ❣ Requires Heart of the Mountain Tier 2.; RARE
The Starfall Seasoning is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Drills|Drill]] upgrade module that can be obtained from forging in [[The Forge]] in the [[Dwarven Mines]].
## Obtaining
The Starfall Seasoning can be forged once the player has reached [[Heart of the Mountain|Heart of the Mountain II (2)]]. It requires 64 (1 stack) [[Starfall]] and 16 [[Treasurite]]. It takes 18 hours to forge.
### Forging Tree
- [[Starfall Seasoning]] 1 Starfall Seasoning
  - 16 [[Treasurite]]
  - 64 [[Starfall]]
## Usage
The Starfall Seasoning can be applied to any [[Drills|Drill]] via [[Drill Anvil]], to increase its stats by +25 [[Mining Speed]] and +10 [[Mining Fortune]], which is doubled within 20 blocks of a [[Fallen Stars|Fallen Star]]. It can then be removed via the same menu for 9,999 [[Coins]], which gives the item back to the player.

Starlight Armor is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Armor]] set. Its Full Set Bonus increases the duration and range of the [[Starlight Wand]]'s Starfall ability.
## Obtaining
The Starlight Armor can be purchased from [[Rosetta]] for 180,000 [[Coins]] after the player has reached [[Combat|Combat IX (9)]].

Tooltip: Ability: Starfall RIGHT CLICK Shower stars in a 3 blocks area for 5s, dealing 300 damage per second to mobs within.; Mana Cost: 100 Cooldown: 2s; ❣ Requires Combat Skill 8.; RARE WAND
The Starlight Wand is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Wands|Wand]]. Its ability can be improved by the [[Starlight Armor]] set.
## Obtaining
The Starlight Wand can be purchased from [[Rosetta]] for 30,000 [[Coins]] after the player has reached [[Combat|Combat IX (9)]].
## Usage
Wand:
Starlight Wand
[Effects]
[Ability: Starfall RIGHT CLICK; Shower stars in a 3 blocks area for 5s, dealing 300 damage per second to mobs within.; Mana Cost (Intelligence): 100; Cooldown: 2s; Intelligence Scaling (Damage Calculation): 0.3]
Right clicking with the Starlight Wand places down a cloud on the position of the caster's crosshair up to 10 blocks away. This cloud has a radius of 3x3 and rains down stars, dealing damage every 0.25 seconds. With the Starlight Armor's Full Set Bonus, the cloud's duration is doubled and the area is increased to 4x4 blocks. It costs 100 [[Mana]] to use and has a cooldown of 2s.

Tooltip: Torrhus Serendipity I Increases the odds of finding rare items in Tree Gifts at Torrhus Canyon by +2%.; You can Syphon this shard from your Hunting Box.; Right-click to send to Hunting Box!; Shift Right-click to move all!; ❣ Requires Hunting Skill 10.; RARE WATER SHARD (ID R83)
The Stingray Shard is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Torrhus Serendipity]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Fishing
A Stingray Shard can be obtained from [[Fishing]] in the [[Torrhus Canyon]] with [[Wooden Bait]]. There is a 100% (1 in 1 catches) chance for [[Treasure|GOOD CATCH]] drops to be replaced with one.
### Huntraps
A Stingray Shard can be obtained from [[Huntraps]] by placing them in the [[Hotspot Haven]] (Underwater) or [[Spring Depths]] (Underwater).
### Fusion
A Stingray Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|ID Fusion]] and [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]].
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Stingray Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Torrhus Serendipity]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Torrhus Serendipity ([[Attributes]]); Increases the odds of finding rare items in [[Tree Gifts]] at [[Torrhus Canyon]] by +2%–20%.
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].
|Level|Shards; Required|Total; Shards|Total; SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|Ⅰ (1)|1|1|+1 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅱ (2)|2|3|+2 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅲ (3)|3|6|+3 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅳ (4)|3|9|+4 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅴ (5)|4|13|+5 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅵ (6)|4|17|+6 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅶ (7)|5|22|+7 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅷ (8)|6|28|+8 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅸ (9)|8|36|+9 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅹ (10)|12|48|+10 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
### Fusion
A Stingray Shard can be used in [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]] to create other [[Attributes|Shards]].

Chameleon-Fusable ([[Attribute Fusion]]): ✔ Yes
[Metadata]
Item ID: ATTRIBUTE_SHARD
Shard Item ID: STONEWORM
Tooltip: Refined Luck I Increases your chance of finding Refined Minerals during Mining Fiestas by +1%. Effects are doubled if you have a Fiesta Flask active!; You can Syphon this shard from your Hunting Box.; Right-click to send to Hunting Box!; Shift Right-click to move all!; ❣ Requires Hunting Skill 10.; RARE COMBAT SHARD (ID R81)
The Stoneworm Shard is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Refined Luck]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Hunting
A Stoneworm Shard can be obtained from killing a [[Stoneworm]] with enough [[Charm Chance]] to [[Charm]] it.
### Fusion
A Stoneworm Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|ID Fusion]] and [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]].
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Stoneworm Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Refined Luck]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Refined Luck ([[Attributes]]); Increases your chance of finding Refined Minerals ([[Refined Mineral]]) during Mining Fiestas ([[Mining Fiesta]]) by +1%–10%. Effects are doubled if you have a [[Fiesta Flask]] active!
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].
|Level|Shards; Required|Total; Shards|Total; SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|Ⅰ (1)|1|1|+1 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅱ (2)|2|3|+2 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅲ (3)|3|6|+3 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅳ (4)|3|9|+4 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅴ (5)|4|13|+5 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅵ (6)|4|17|+6 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅶ (7)|5|22|+7 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅷ (8)|6|28|+8 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅸ (9)|8|36|+9 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅹ (10)|12|48|+10 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
### Fusion
A Stoneworm Shard can be used in [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]] to create other [[Attributes|Shards]].

## Lore
When viewed in the [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] or [[Bazaar]], the Stoneworm Shard has the following description:
- Its tough skin and strong teeth allow it to burrow through stone as if it were sand.

# Strength Elixir
Categories: Rare items, Items
[Strength Elixir]
Type: Item
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: RIFT_STRENGTH_ELIXIR
Tooltip: Grants Strength (10:00), granting +3 Rift Damage.; RARE
The Strength Elixir is a [[Rarity|RARE]] item.
## Obtaining
The Strength Elixir can be purchased from [[Shifted]] for 5 [[Shy Crux|Shy Cruxes]] and 12,000 [[Motes]].
## Usage
The player can consume a Strength Elixir to grant [[Strength Potion|Strength]] for 10:00 and is the only way to gain the effect in the [[Rift Dimension]] since [[Potions]] cannot be transferred.
The effect provided by the Strength Elixir grants +3 [[Rift Damage]].
## Bugs
- The [[Rift Damage]] given by the Strength Elixir often stops working sometime when the potion still has its effect.
## Notes
- The effect doesn't show up in the Effects menu.
## History

# Summoning Ring
Categories: Rare items, Items, Catacombs Loot, Catacombs, Dungeons, Dungeon Items, Necromancy

Type: Dungeon Item ([[Dungeon Items]])
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Enchantable: ✕ No
Reforgeable: ✕ No
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No

Auctionable: ✔ Yes

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: SUMMONING_RING
Tooltip: Ability: Raise Souls RIGHT CLICK Monsters you kill while you have this item will drop their soul.; You can click on their souls on the ground using this item to absorb them and then spawn them to fight by your side.; Mana cost is based on the power of the monsters that you summon.; Shift right-click to view and remove souls from this item. If your summoned monster dies the soul will be removed.; Cooldown: 1s; Max Souls: 2; RARE DUNGEON ITEM
The Summoning Ring is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Dungeon Items|Dungeon Item]] that can be used to absorb 2 souls of [[Mob|Mobs]] you kill. You can then summon these mobs using [[Mana]], the [[Mana]] cost depending on the power of the mobs. It is a [[Necromancy]] item.
## Obtaining
### Dungeon Reward Chests
The Summoning Ring can be obtained from [[Dungeon Reward Chest|Dungeon Reward Chests]] in [[The Catacombs - Floor VI]].
|Catacombs Floor ([[Catacombs]])|Chest ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]])|Cost; (Chest + Added)|Average Chance; (No Bonuses)|Average Chance; (S+ (Score above 300 points.) ([[Dungeon Score]]) Max Bonuses ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]]))|Quality ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]])|Weight ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]])|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|Floor VI ([[The Catacombs - Floor VI]])|Obsidian|12,000,000 [[Coins]]|0.2237% (1 in 447 runs)|0.3695% (1 in 270.6 runs)|310|1|
|Floor VI ([[The Catacombs - Floor VI]])|Bedrock|12,000,000 [[Coins]]|0.3751% (1 in 266.6 runs)|0.6767% (1 in 147.8 runs)|310|10|
|Floor VI ([[The Catacombs - Floor VI]]) (Master Mode)|Obsidian|12,000,000 [[Coins]]|0.2193% (1 in 456 runs)|0.4019% (1 in 248.8 runs)|310|1|
|Floor VI ([[The Catacombs - Floor VI]]) (Master Mode)|Bedrock|12,000,000 [[Coins]]|0.6624% (1 in 151 runs)|0.7192% (1 in 139 runs)|310|10|
## Usage
Item:
Summoning Ring
[Effects]
[Ability: Raise Souls RIGHT CLICK; Monsters you kill using this item will drop their soul. You can click on their souls on the ground using this item to absorb them and then spawn them to fight by your side.; Mana cost is based on the power of the monsters that you summon. Shift right-click to view and remove souls from this item. If your summoned monster dies the soul will be removed.; Cooldown: 1s; Max Souls (Necromancy): 2]
## Trivia
- It has the same texture as the [[Ring of Love]], but the gem on the top of the ring is red instead of green.
- The maximum number of Souls the Summoning Ring can store can be increased through the [[Reaper Mask]] (granting +2 slots), [[Necromancer Lord Armor]] (granting +1 slot per piece), and [[Ghoul Pet]] (granting +2 slots).
- This item can be used to easily transport the [[The Loch Emperor|Loch Emperor]] to Jake's House to obtain the [[Golden Ball]].
- Necromancy mobs spawned in the [[Dragon's Nest]] will despawn after an [[Ender Dragon]] has been defeated.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Oct 1, 2020|0.9.4|Added Summoning Ring.|

Item ID: GOBLIN_OMELETTE_SUNNY_SIDE
Tooltip: Drill Part; Grants +50 Mining Fortune, but this Drill consumes quadruple the Fuel.; Put this item in the Upgrade Module slot of a Drill at a Drill Mechanic!; May cause inflammation; ❣ Requires Heart of the Mountain Tier 5.; RARE
Sunny Side Goblin Omelette is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Drills|Drill]] upgrade module that can be obtained from forging in [[The Forge]] in the [[Dwarven Mines]]. It grants +50 [[Mining Fortune]], but quadruples the [[Machine Fuel|Drill Fuel]] consumption.
## Obtaining
The Sunny Side Goblin Omelette can be forged once the player has reached [[Heart of the Mountain|Heart of the Mountain V (5)]]. It requires 96 (1 stack plus 32) [[Yellow Goblin Egg|Yellow Goblin Eggs]], 1 [[Goblin Omelette]], 1 [[Gemstone|Flawless Topaz Gemstone]], and 20 hours to forge.
### Forging Tree
- [[Sunny Side Goblin Omelette]] 1 Sunny Side Goblin Omelette
  - 96 [[Yellow Goblin Egg]]
  - 1 [[Gemstone|Flawless Topaz Gemstone]] 80 (1 stack plus 16) [[Gemstone|Fine Topaz Gemstone]] 6,400 (100 stacks) [[Gemstone|Flawed Topaz Gemstone]] 512,000 (8,000 stacks) [[Gemstone|Rough Topaz Gemstone]]
  - 1 [[Goblin Omelette]] 96 [[Goblin Egg]]
## Usage
The Sunny Side Goblin Omelette can be applied to any [[Drills|Drill]] via [[Drill Anvil]], to grant it 50 [[Mining Fortune]], but also make it consume 4x as much Fuel. It can then be removed via the same menu for 9,999 [[Coins]], which gives the item back to the player.
## Notes
- Mining high tier Gemstones normally consumes 2 Fuel each, so the Sunny Side Goblin Omelette will cause it to consume 8 Fuel each. Mining [[Glacite]], [[Umber]] or [[Tungsten]] will cause the Drill to consume 12 Fuel each.

Tooltip: Reforge Stone Combinable in Anvil; Applies the Sunny reforge when combined with Armor.; Grants the following stats that scale with the item's rarity:;  Speed ☯ Farming Wisdom; Right-click to view rarity scaling!; ❣ Requires Mining Skill 11.; RARE REFORGE STONE
A Sunstone is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Reforge Stones|Reforge Stone]] which can apply the [[Reforging|Sunny]] [[Reforging|Reforge]] to [[Armor]]. It is unlocked at [[Sunflower|Sunflower VI (6)]]. It grants [[Speed]] and [[Farming Wisdom]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Sunflower VI (6) ([[Sunflower]])|8x [[Compacted Sunflower]] 8x Fine Topaz Gemstone ([[Gemstone]])|Recipe: [[Compacted Sunflower]] ×8 + [[Gemstone]] x8 → Sunstone|
## Usage
### Reforging
Sunstone can be applied to [[Armor]] using an [[Anvil]] or [[The Hex]] to apply the [[Reforging|Sunny]] reforge, to grant [[Speed]] and [[Farming Wisdom]], which scale with the armor's rarity. This has a skill requirement of [[Mining|Mining XI (11)]] and can be done with any armor, regardless of the piece. Armor that is already reforged to [[Reforging|Sunny]] will grant the stats, even if the player doesn't meet the requirement to apply it.

|Cost to apply|100,000 [[Coins]]|100,000 [[Coins]]|100,000 [[Coins]]|100,000 [[Coins]]|100,000 [[Coins]]|100,000 [[Coins]]|100,000 [[Coins]]|

Tooltip: Gear Score: 141 (155); Damage: +105 (+115.5); Strength: +20 (+22); Crit Damage: +20% (+22%); Ability: Cleave When hitting an entity, monsters in a 3 block range will be hit for a portion of that damage too.; This item can be reforged!; RARE DUNGEON SWORD
The Super Cleaver is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Dungeons|Dungeon]] [[Swords|Sword]].
## Obtaining
A Super Cleaver can be purchased from [[Ophelia]] for 80,000 [[Coins]] and 1 [[Cleaver]].
## Usage
Sword:
Super Cleaver [[Damage]]: +105 [[Strength]]: +20 [[Crit Damage]]: +20
[Effects]
[Ability: Cleave; When hitting an entity, monsters in a 3 block range will be hit for a portion of that damage too.]
The Super Cleaver, being a sword, can be used as a melee weapon. Its Cleave ability causes mobs within a radius of 3 blocks to take a portion of damage dealt to the wielder's target.
### Merchants
A Super Cleaver alongside 800,000 [[Coins]] is required to purchase [[Hyper Cleaver]] from [[Ophelia]].
## Essence Crafting
Essence Required; [[Gold Essence]]:
|Action|Cost|Cumul. (Cumulative)|
|-|-|-|
|✪|1|1|
|✪✪|1|2|
|✪✪✪|2|4|
|✪✪✪✪|2; 10,000 [[Coins]]|6; 10,000 [[Coins]]|
|✪✪✪✪✪|3; 25,000 [[Coins]]|9; 35,000 [[Coins]]|
## Tips
- The Super Cleaver a great weapon for the early game, being very accessible thanks to the low price and no requirements. It is useful both inside and outside of [[Dungeons]], and while it may seem worse than something such as an [[Aspect of the Dragons]], it is much more recommended to use a Super Cleaver and upgrade it as you progress through the [[Catacombs]].
## Trivia
- The item ability item effect#Cleave Cleave from League of Legends shares a name as and possesses a similar effect to the Super Cleaver.

# Super Compactor 3000
Aliases: SC3000
Categories: Rare items, Items, Craftable items, Crafting ingredients, Cobblestone Collection, Minion Modifiers
[Super Compactor 3000]
Also known as: SC3000 SC3K Super Compactor
Type: Item
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
Collection ([[Collections]]): Cobblestone X (10) ([[Cobblestone]])
Function: Compacts items produced by minions into their enchanted form.
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✔ Yes
[Shop]
Sell: 50,000 [[Coins]]
[Materials]
Raw Materials (All materials in their most basic form needed to obtain the item.): 36,960x (577 stacks plus 32 (36,960 in total)) [[Cobblestone]] 5,280x (82 stacks plus 32 (5,280 in total)) Redstone ([[Redstone Dust]])
Raw Materials to upgrade: 35,840x (560 stacks (35,840 items total)) [[Cobblestone]] 5,120x (80 stacks (5,120 items total)) Redstone ([[Redstone Dust]])
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: SUPER_COMPACTOR_3000
Tooltip: Minion Upgrade; This item can be used as a minion upgrade. This will automatically turn materials that a minion produces into their enchanted form when there are enough resources in the minion's storage.; RARE
Super Compactor 3000 is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Minion Upgrades|Minion upgrade]] unlocked at [[Cobblestone|Cobblestone X (10)]]. The upgrade is similar to the [[Compactor]], however instead of compressing items into blocks, they will turn into their enchanted form if enough materials are available.
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Cobblestone X (10) ([[Cobblestone]])|1x [[Compactor]] 32x [[Enchanted Redstone Dust]] 224x (3 stacks plus 32 (224 in total)) [[Enchanted Cobblestone]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Cobblestone]] x32 ×7 + [[Compactor]] + [[Enchanted Redstone Dust]] x32 → Super Compactor 3000|
## Usage
### Minion Upgrade
Super Compactor 3000 can be placed in all [[Minions]]. It compacts all the items in the minion's inventory once the minion makes produce. This means that Super Compactor 3000 doesn't compact if it is given to a minion with full inventory.
Although not mentioned in their item description, it can also compact enchanted items into their further compacted forms (such as [[Enchanted Raw Porkchop]] to [[Enchanted Cooked Porkchop]]).
Players are not required to have the recipe of the enchanted item or enchanted block form of the item unlocked for their minion equipped with the Super Compactor 3000 to produce one.
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Personal Compactor 4000]]|Redstone Dust VI (6) ([[Redstone Dust]])|[[Super Compactor 3000]] 1x Super Compactor 3000 28x [[Enchanted Redstone Dust]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Redstone Dust]] x4 ×7 + Super Compactor 3000 → [[Personal Compactor 4000]]|
|[[Dwarven Super Compactor]]|Mithril VI (6) ([[Mithril]])|[[Super Compactor 3000]] 1x Super Compactor 3000 1x [[Auto Smelter]] 56x [[Enchanted Mithril]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Mithril]] x8 ×7 + [[Auto Smelter]] + Super Compactor 3000 → [[Dwarven Super Compactor]]|
## Trivia
- Players used to be able to purchase [[Cobblestone]] from the [[Mining Merchant]] for 215,040 [[Coins]], but this is now really inefficient due to the addition of [[Bazaar]] and 640 of each unique item from [[NPC|NPCs]] / day limitation.
- Upgrading a minion with Super Compactor 3000 grants [[Achievements|"Time to go on vacation." Achievement]], reference to its ability to greatly "increase" the storage capacity of a minion and thus how long it can work for before being full.
- Super Compactor 3000 can compact items that have been turned into their block form, which means it can be used with a [[Compactor]], although it's not recommended.
- The [[Dwarven Super Compactor]] has the exact same function as using both a [[Super Compactor 3000]] Super Compactor 3000 and an [[Auto Smelter]], with the added benefit that it uses only one slot instead of both. However, it is more expensive, costing an extra 56 [[Enchanted Mithril]] (or 8,960 (140 stacks) [[Mithril]]) to craft.
## History

|Sep 11, 2019|0.7.1|Now works with [[Wool]], [[Fishing Minion]], and [[Flower Minion]].|
|Oct 11, 2019|0.7.2|Now correctly turns [[Enchanted Potato]] into [[Enchanted Baked Potato]].|
|Feb 21, 2020|0.7.6|Now works with [[Cactus Minion]] and [[Snowball]].|
|Jun 2, 2020|0.7.9|Added conversion from [[Enchanted Ice]] into [[Enchanted Packed Ice]].|
|Aug 24, 2021|Added new conversions (mainly enchanted blocks).|
|Jul 12, 2023|0.19.1|Fixed being able to sell Co-op Soulbound ([[Soulbound]]) Super Compactor 3000s in the Bazaar.|

Tooltip: Consumed on use; Can be used to remove Pet Items from your active Pet. You will be refunded the Pet Item.; &quot;What is the benefit of outright removing a pet item instead of swapping it with another, you ask?; Well, uh, it...looks...nicer?&quot; - Plumber Joe; Right-click on your summoned pet to remove its item!; RARE
A Super Scrubber is a [[Rarity|RARE]] Item that removes [[Pet Items]].
## Obtaining
A Super Scrubber can be purchased from [[Plumber Joe]] for 25,000 [[Coins]].
## Usage
Right-clicking a [[Pets|Pet]] whilst holding a Super Scrubber will open a menu to confirm removing the pet item. Removing a pet item costs a varying amount of [[Coins]] depending on the rarity of the pet item, and consumes the Super Scrubber.
|Rarity|Cost|
|-|-|
|COMMON ([[Rarity]])|25,000 [[Coins]]|
|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|50,000 [[Coins]]|
|RARE ([[Rarity]])|100,000 [[Coins]]|
|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|250,000 [[Coins]]|
|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|500,000 [[Coins]]|
|MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])|1,000,000 [[Coins]]|
|DIVINE ([[Rarity]])|2,500,000 [[Coins]]|
Attempting to remove a [[Pet Items|Pet Item]] when one isn't held results in the following message:
Your Pet isn't holding any item!
## History

[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Jan 27, 2026|0.24.1|Added Super Scrubber.|

# Superboom TNT
Categories: Rare items, Items in Sacks, Blocks, Catacombs, Dungeons, Tools, Other Tools, Dungeon Items
[Superboom TNT]
Type: Block
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
Obtained: [[Walter]], [[Chunk]] or [[Ophelia]] stock
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Sacks: [[Dungeon Sack]]
[Shop]
Merchants: [[Walter]] [[Chunk]] [[Ophelia]]
Buy: 2,500 [[Coins]]; 3,000 [[Coins]]
Sell: 2 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: SUPERBOOM_TNT
Tooltip: Blows up cracked brick walls and crypts, which are typically found in Dungeons and the Crystal Hollows.; RARE
Superboom TNT is a [[Rarity|RARE]] item that can be placed down by the player in order to break [[Cracked Wall|Weak Walls]], marked by Cracked Stone Bricks. Upon using Superboom TNT, the item is consumed.
## Obtaining
### Purchase
Superboom TNT is sold for 2,500 [[Coins]] by [[Walter]] in the back of the [[Gunpowder Mines]], and by [[Chunk]] for 2,500 [[Coins]] in the [[Crystal Hollows]]. [[Ophelia]] sells Superboom TNT for 3,000 [[Coins]] at the [[Catacombs Entrance]] or the [[Dungeon Hub]].
### Mob Drops
Superboom TNT can also be obtained by killing [[Angry Archaeologist|Angry Archaeologists]], [[Lost Adventurer|Lost Adventurers]], [[Frozen Adventurer|Frozen Adventurers]], [[The Watcher|Boomers]] in the [[The Catacombs - Floor V|Floor 5]] [[Magical Map|Blood Room]], and the final mob in a dungeons room.
## Usage
When placed, the Superboom TNT can open secret areas in:
|Zone|Leads to|
|-|-|
|[[Coal Mine]]|[[Crypts]]|
|[[Dark Thicket]]|[[Howling Cave]]|
|[[Savanna Woodland]]|[[Soul Cave]]|
|[[Jungle Island]]|[[Spirit Cave]]|
|[[Gunpowder Mines]]|Leads to a Fairy Soul ([[Fairy Souls]])|
|[[Gravel Mines]]|[[Arachne's Burrow]]|
|The Catacombs ([[Catacombs]])|Leads to several Secrets ([[Dungeon Secrets]])|
Once an area has been opened by a player, it will be accessible by the rest of the server for 30 seconds, after which the wall will close again. Once closed, if the player is inside, the only way to get out is to warp to the [[Private Island]] (or another island if the player has the [[Travel Scrolls|Travel Scroll]]), use another Superboom TNT (or wait for someone else to use one), or use a [[Horse Pet]] to glitch out.
### Crystal Hollows
Inside the [[Crystal Hollows]], Superboom TNT can be used to blow up the two tombs in one of [[Boss Corleone]] spawning rooms, it will reveal a chest and spawn a [[Crypt Undead]].
### Dungeons
Inside [[Dungeons]], Superboom TNT can be used to blow up tombs. Different mobs can spawn from different tombs:
- [[Crypt Undead]] (from normal tombs in almost every room)
- [[King Midas]] (from golden tombs)
- [[Prince]] (from hidden or special tomb)
- Admin skinned Crypt Undead (from tombs inside the Admin Room)
- [[Deathmite]] (from tomb in lava in a unique room, there is a sign above it that reads "Here Lies an Ancient Evil")
Superboom TNT can also be used to blow up cracked walls, usually hiding secrets.
The [[Archer]]'s Explosive Shot class ability, the [[Mage]]'s Guided Sheep class ability and the [[Conjuring]]'s ability can alternatively be used to blow up anything Superboom TNT can.
## Trivia
- Superboom TNT cannot be used in the [[Dungeon Hub]], [[Private Island]], and [[The Garden]]. Attempting to use it results in the message: Cannot use this item here!
- Secret areas could be glitched into without using the Superboom TNT by spawning the [[Horse Pet]] when against the cracked stone wall, ride it and head into the secret area (does not work in dungeons due to mounts being disabled).
  - This is now not possible due to the [[Horse Pet]] no longer being a mount.
- If the player's inventory is full, but they have space for a Superboom TNT, and they try to pick up a Superboom TNT dropped from a [[Minibosses|Miniboss]], then it will say "You don't have enough space in your inventory to pick up this item!" However, if they drop an item, then pick up the Superboom TNT, it will then stack with the Superboom TNT in their inventory.
- The Superboom TNT is likely inspired from The Pit's Instaboom TNT.

Superlite Motor is a [[Rarity|RARE]] item and one of the items required to obtain the [[Gemstone Crystals|Sapphire Crystal]].
## Obtaining
### Mob Drops
|Level|Mob|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[Lv71]|[[Automaton]]|1|4Uncommon Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|2%|
|[Lv67]|[[Automaton]]|1|6Rare Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|1%|
### Treasure Chests
A Superlite Motor can also be found as an drop from treasure chests within the [[Precursor Remnants]]. It has a 0.34% chance to roll as a drop in common loot chests, and a 0.29% chance to roll as a drop in rare loot chests.
## Usage
### Sapphire Gemstone Crystal
Bringing a Superlite Motor and the 5 other [[Automaton]] components to [[Professor Robot]] in the [[Lost Precursor City]] rewards the player with the [[Gemstone Crystals|Sapphire Crystal]].

# Suspicious Scrap
Aliases: Scrap
Categories: Rare items, Items in Sacks, Items, Confirmations needed, Dwarven Mines
[Suspicious Scrap]
Type: Item
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Enchantable: ✕ No
Reforgeable: ✕ No
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Sacks: [[Dwarven Sack]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: SUSPICIOUS_SCRAP
Tooltip: Found within the deepest depths of the Glacite Tunnels. Place in the Fossil Excavator to begin excavating!; It's pretty heavy... maybe something valuable lies within?; RARE
A Suspicious Scrap is a [[Rarity|RARE]] item that can be used in the [[Fossil Excavator]] to uncover treasure.
## Obtaining
### Mining
Suspicious Scrap has a base chance of 0.5% (1 in 200 blocks broken) chance of dropping when mining [[Ores]], [[Dwarven Metals]], and [[Gemstone|Gemstones]] while in [[Glacite Mineshafts]]. Each time a Suspicious Scrap is obtained, the chance to obtain another one is halved.
The chance can be calculated using the following formula, where n is the number of scraps obtained so far:
  Chance=200×2n
Examples:
|#|Chance|
|-|-|
|1|0.5% (1 in 200 blocks broken)|
|2|0.25% (1 in 400 blocks broken)|
|3|0.125% (1 in 800 blocks broken)|
|4|0.0625% (1 in 1600 blocks broken)|
|4|0.03125% (1 in 3200 blocks broken)|
|5|0.01562% (1 in 6400 blocks broken)|
|6|0.007812% (1 in 12,800 blocks broken)|
|7|0.003906% (1 in 25,600 blocks broken)|
|8|0.001953% (1 in 51,200 blocks broken)|
|9|0.0009766% (1 in 102,400 blocks broken)|
|10|0.0004883% (1 in 204,800 blocks broken)|
There are 4 possible buffs that increase the chance for Suspicious Scrap, all of which are multiplicative [Confirm (Confirmation needed; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)].
|Buff|Chance|
|-|-|
|Paleontologist I–V ([[Paleontologist]])|2.5%–12.5%|
|[[Heart of the Mountain]]'s No Stone Unturned perk|0.5%–25%|
|[[Heart of the Mountain]]'s Mineshaft Mayhem Perk|5%|
|Scrap Chance I-X ([[Attributes]])|1%–10%|
#### Game Message
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|Suspicious Scrap found|EXCAVATOR! You found a Suspicious Scrap!|
### Frozen Corpses
Suspicious Scrap can also be obtained as a drop from [[Frozen Corpses]]. This does not reduce the chance of obtaining Suspicious Scrap from mining.
|Type|Quantity|Chance Per Roll|
|-|-|-|
|Lapis|[[Suspicious Scrap]] 1 Suspicious Scrap|2.99% (1 in 33.5)|
|Umber Tungsten|[[Suspicious Scrap]] 2 Suspicious Scrap|10.7% (1 in 9.34)|
|Umber Tungsten|[[Suspicious Scrap]] 4 Suspicious Scrap|5.35% (1 in 18.69)|
|Vanguard|[[Suspicious Scrap]] 8 Suspicious Scrap|4.55% (1 in 21.97)|
|Vanguard|[[Suspicious Scrap]] 16 Suspicious Scrap|2.28% (1 in 43.94)|
### Purchasing
Suspicious Scrap can also be purchased for 100 (1 stack plus 36) [[Fossil Essence]] at the [[Essence Shops|Fossil Essence Shop]].
## Usage
Suspicious Scrap can be put into the [[Fossil Excavator]] along with a [[Chisel]] to get a variety of rewards, including [[Fossils]], [[Fossil Essence]], [[Gemstone|Gemstones]], ᠅ [[Glacite Powder]], [[Gold Essence]], [[Diamond Essence]], [[Enchanted Glacite]], [[Enchanted Tungsten]], [[Enchanted Umber]], [[Fossil the Fish]], and [[Paleontologist|Paleontologist I]].
Suspicious Scrap can also be salvaged for 5 [[Fossil Essence]].

The Suspicious Vial is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Reforge Stones|Reforge Stone]], which can apply the [[Reforging|Suspicious]] [[Reforging|Reforge]] to a [[Weapons|melee weapon]], granting [[Damage]], [[Crit Chance]] and [[Crit Damage]].
## Obtaining
### Boss Collection
A Suspicious Vial can be claimed once as a reward from [[The Professor|The Professor I (1)]], which is unlocked after defeating [[The Professor]] 25 times.
### Dungeon Reward Chests
The Suspicious Vial can be dropped from [[Dungeon Reward Chest|Dungeon Reward Chests]] on [[The Catacombs - Floor III]].
|Catacombs Floor ([[Catacombs]])|Chest ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]])|Cost; (Chest + Added)|Average Chance; (No Bonuses)|Average Chance; (S+ (Score above 300 points.) ([[Dungeon Score]]) Max Bonuses ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]]))|Quality ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]])|Weight ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]])|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|Floor III ([[The Catacombs - Floor III]])|Diamond|250,000 [[Coins]]|3.2258% (1 in 31 runs)|6.4952% (1 in 15.4 runs)|145|5|
|Floor III ([[The Catacombs - Floor III]])|Emerald|500,000 [[Coins]]|6.4198% (1 in 15.6 runs)|8.8112% (1 in 11.3 runs)|145|5|
|Floor III ([[The Catacombs - Floor III]])|Obsidian|1,000,000 [[Coins]]|11.6611% (1 in 8.6 runs)|16.8024% (1 in 6 runs)|145|5|

## Usage
### Reforging
Suspicious Vial can be applied to [[Swords]], [[Longswords]] and [[Gemstone Gauntlet]] using an [[Anvil]] or [[The Hex]] to apply the [[Reforging|Suspicious]] reforge, to grant [[Damage]], [[Crit Chance]], and [[Crit Damage]], which scale with the weapon's rarity. This has a skill requirement of [[Mining|Mining XXIII (23)]]. Weapon that is already reforged to [[Reforging|Suspicious]] will grant the stats, even if the player doesn't meet the requirement to apply it.
|Stat Bonus|Rarity COMMON ([[Rarity]])|Rarity UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|Rarity RARE ([[Rarity]])|Rarity EPIC ([[Rarity]])|Rarity LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|Rarity MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])|Rarity DIVINE ([[Rarity]])|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|Stat Bonus|+5 Dmg ([[Damage]]) +1 CC ([[Crit Chance]]) +30 CD ([[Crit Damage]])|+7 Dmg ([[Damage]]) +2 CC ([[Crit Chance]]) +40 CD ([[Crit Damage]])|+9 Dmg ([[Damage]]) +3 CC ([[Crit Chance]]) +50 CD ([[Crit Damage]])|+11 Dmg ([[Damage]]) +5 CC ([[Crit Chance]]) +65 CD ([[Crit Damage]])|+13 Dmg ([[Damage]]) +7 CC ([[Crit Chance]]) +85 CD ([[Crit Damage]])|+15 Dmg ([[Damage]]) +10 CC ([[Crit Chance]]) +110 CD ([[Crit Damage]])|+17 Dmg ([[Damage]]) +13 CC ([[Crit Chance]]) +140 CD ([[Crit Damage]])|
|Cost to apply|100,000 [[Coins]]|100,000 [[Coins]]|100,000 [[Coins]]|100,000 [[Coins]]|100,000 [[Coins]]|100,000 [[Coins]]|100,000 [[Coins]]|
## Tips
- [[Reforging|Suspicious]] should generally provide better damage on average than [[Reforging|Fabled]] on [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] weapons that have less than 200 [[Damage]] (after applying [[Hot Potato Book|Hot Potato Books]] and [[Fuming Potato Book|Fuming Potato Books]], if any).
  - However, the balance of [[Strength]] and [[Crit Damage]] may change this slightly, therefore it is better to test or calculate the damage in each individual situation.
## Trivia
- Suspicious is the best reforge for [[Crit Damage]], which made it commonly paired with [[Scylla]] for its Wither Shield and [[Mastiff Armor]] before they got reworked.

The Sylvan Shard is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Forest Elemental]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Fusion
A Sylvan Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]] and [[Attribute Fusion|Special Fusion]]. For [[Attribute Fusion|Special Fusion]], the following input conditions must be met:
- First: [[Rarity|RARE]] and Forest Category
- Second: [[Rarity|EPIC]] or higher
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Sylvan Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Forest Elemental]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Forest Elemental ([[Attributes]]); Grants +2–20 [[Health]].
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].

[Synthetic Heart]
Type: Item
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]

[Item Metadata]

Synthetic Heart is a [[Rarity|RARE]] item and one of the items required to obtain the [[Gemstone Crystals|Sapphire Crystal]].
## Obtaining
### Mob Drops
|Level|Mob|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[Lv71]|[[Automaton]]|1|4Uncommon Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|2%|
|[Lv67]|[[Automaton]]|1|6Rare Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|1%|
### Treasure Chests
A Synthetic Heart can also be found as an drop from treasure chests within the [[Precursor Remnants]]. It has a 0.34% chance to roll as a drop in common loot chests, and a 0.29% chance to roll as a drop in rare loot chests.
## Usage
### Sapphire Gemstone Crystal
Bringing a Synthetic Heart and the 5 other [[Automaton]] components to [[Professor Robot]] in the [[Lost Precursor City]] rewards the player with the [[Gemstone Crystals|Sapphire Crystal]].
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Precursor Apparatus]]|None|1x [[Electron Transmitter]] 1x [[Superlite Motor]] 1x [[FTX 3070]] 1x [[Robotron Reflector]] 1x [[Control Switch]] [[Synthetic Heart]] 1x Synthetic Heart|Recipe: [[Control Switch]] + [[Electron Transmitter]] + [[FTX 3070]] + [[Robotron Reflector]] + [[Superlite Motor]] + Synthetic Heart → [[Precursor Apparatus]]|
## Trivia
- The Synthetic Heart is most likely based off the Centurion Dynamo Core from The Elder Scrolls V: Skyrim.

Talbot's Theodolite is a [[Rarity|RARE]] tool bought from [[Talbot]] that allows the [[Player]] to find special animals on the [[Mushroom Desert]].
## Obtaining
### Shop Purchase
Talbot's Theodolite can be purchased from [[Talbot]] for 80 [[Pelts]].
## Usage
Item:
Talbot's Theodolite
[Effects]
[Ability: Track RIGHT CLICK; Indicates the approximate height difference and angle between you and your tracked mob.; Mana Cost (Intelligence): 100; Cooldown: 10s]
Talbot's Theodolite is used to locate special animals. When used on the [[Mushroom Desert]], the Track ability will tell the player if a tracker animal is above or below them, as well as the angle at which the animal is located.

# Tasty Cat Food
Categories: Rare items, Items
[Tasty Cat Food]
Type: Item
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Rift Transferrable ([[Rift Dimension]]): ✔ Yes
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: DEAD_CAT_FOOD
Tooltip: Use on a mythological cat to transform this item.; Pss-pss-pss-pss Good cats only!; X Rift-Transferable X RARE PET ITEM
Tasty Cat Food is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Pet Items|Pet Item]]. It is [[Rift Dimension|# Rift-Transferable #]].
## Obtaining
Tasty Cat Food can be obtained by transferring a [[Dead Cat Food]], which can be purchased from [[Barrier Street]], from the [[Rift Dimension|Rift]] to the [[Hub Island|Hub]].
Tasty Cat Food can also be obtained as a MAJOR reward from [[Raffle of the Century]].
## Usage
Tasty Cat Food can be used on [[Siamese Lynxes]] to convert it into a [[Pre-Digestion Fish]].
### Transfer
Due to it being [[Rift Dimension|# Rift-Transferable #]], Tasty Cat Food can also be transferred to the [[Rift Dimension]] through the Rift Transfer Chest behind the [[Dark Auction]]. Once in the [[Rift Dimension|Rift]], Tasty Cat Food will be converted into [[Dead Cat Food]].
## Trivia
- Despite being classed as a [[Pet Items|Pet Item]], Tasty Cat Food cannot be applied to any [[Pets|Pet]], unlike its Rift counterpart.
- It is the only item that is created by transferring an item from the [[Rift Dimension]].
- Feeding [[Siamese Lynxes]] automatically views the recipes for [[Pre-Digestion Fish]], since you need to right-click to feed the Siamese Lynxes.
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|May 4, 2023|Added Tasty Cat Food.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Jun 20, 2023|0.19|Added Tasty Cat Food.|
Linked from: [[Dead Cat Food]], [[Pre-Digestion Fish]], [[Raffle of the Century]], [[Rift Dimension]], [[Siamese Lynxes]]

# Teleporter Pill
Aliases: Teleport Pill
Categories: Rare items, Items, Craftable items, Slayer, Blaze Slayer
[Teleporter Pill]
Type: Item
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
Slayer Level ([[Slayer]]) Requirement: Blaze Slayer IV (4) ([[Blaze Slayer]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Materials]
Raw Materials (All materials in their most basic form needed to obtain the item.): 512x (8 stacks (512 items total)) [[Derelict Ashe]] 2,560x (40 stacks (2,560 items total)) [[Red Sand]] 25,600x (400 stacks (25,600 items total)) [[Mycelium]] 10,240x (160 stacks (10,240 items total)) [[Magma Cream]] 10,240x [[Glowstone Dust]] 10,240x [[Nether Wart]] 10,240x [[Blaze Rod]] 20,480x (320 stacks (20,480 items total)) [[Sugar Cane]] 10,240x [[Ender Pearl]] 2x [[Inferno Vertex]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: TELEPORTER_PILL
Tooltip: Right-click to consume a really nice, really good pill.; Consuming it allows you to use teleport pads on your private island from anywhere.; ☠ Requires Blaze Slayer 4.; RARE
A Teleporter Pill is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Consumables|Permanent Consumable]]. It is unlocked at [[Blaze Slayer|Blaze Slayer IV (4)]].
Consuming it permanently unlocks the ability for the player to teleport to their [[Private Island]] [[Teleport Pad|Teleport Pads]] from anywhere on the [[Private Island]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Blaze Slayer IV (4) ([[Blaze Slayer]])|2x [[Inferno Vertex]] 4x [[Molten Powder]] 64x [[Teleport Pad]] 128x (2 stacks (128 items total)) [[Enchanted Sugar]]|Recipe: [[Molten Powder]] ×4 + [[Enchanted Sugar]] x64 ×2 + [[Inferno Vertex]] ×2 + [[Teleport Pad]] x64 → Teleporter Pill|
## Usage
Right-clicking a Teleporter Pill consumes it, unlocking the Teleporter Pads menu, a menu which allows the player to teleport to any [[Teleport Pad]] on the player's own [[Private Island]] while on it.
The menu can be accessed from the SkyBlock Menu or via /teleports It can only be opened while the player is on their [[Private Island]].
## Trivia
- The Teleporter Pill has the same texture as the [[Placebo Bracelet]].
- The Teleporter Pill previously required 128 (2 stacks) [[Enchanted Cake|Enchanted Cakes]], making it uncraftable even with [[Crafting|Quick Crafting]] due to [[Enchanted Cake|Enchanted Cakes]] being unstackable.
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Mar 8, 2022|Added Teleporter Pill.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Apr 20, 2022|0.13|Added Teleporter Pill.|
|Apr 29, 2022|Changed the recipe to using 128x [[Enchanted Sugar]] instead of 128x [[Enchanted Cake]], allowing the item to be quick crafted.|
|Feb 14, 2023|0.18|Updated the description to reflect being able to work on the Garden ([[The Garden]]).|
|May 22, 2023|No longer works on the Garden ([[The Garden]]).|

[Shop]
Merchants: [[Terry]]
Sell: 1 Coin ([[Coins]])
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: TERRY_SNOWGLOBE
Tooltip: Reforge Stone Combinable in Anvil; Applies the Snowy reforge when combined with Equipment.; Grants the following stats that scale with the item's rarity:;  Fishing Speed  Sea Creature Chance; Right-click to view rarity scaling!; ❣ Requires Mining Skill 15.; RARE REFORGE STONE
The Terry's Snowglobe is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Reforge Stones|Reforge Stone]] which can apply the [[Reforging|Snowy]] [[Reforging|Reforge]] to [[Equipment]], which grants [[Fishing Speed]] and [[Sea Creature Chance]].
## Obtaining
The Terry's Snowglobe can be purchased from [[Terry]] on [[Jerry's Workshop]] for 32 [[Hunk of Ice]] and 8 [[Hunk of Blue Ice]].
## Usage
### Reforging
Terry's Snowglobe can be applied to [[Equipment]] using an [[Anvil]] or [[The Hex]] to apply the [[Reforging|Snowy]] reforge, to grant [[Fishing Speed]] and [[Sea Creature Chance]], which scale with the equipment's rarity. This has a skill requirement of [[Mining|Mining XV (15)]] and can be done with any equipment, regardless of the piece. Equipment that is already reforged to [[Reforging|Snowy]] will grant the stats, even if the player doesn't meet the requirement to apply it.

The Alchemist's Staff is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Wands (disambiguation)|Wand]].
## Obtaining
The Alchemist's Staff is obtained by giving several requested items listed below to [[Baar]] or [[Alchemage]]. Alongside The Alchemist's Staff, players will get 250 Reputation for their respective faction.
Items required:
- 64x [[Gold Ingot]]
- 5x [[Golden Plate]]
- 1x [[Diver's Armor|Diver's Boots]] (Baar) or 1x [[Diver's Armor|Diver's Mask]] (Alchemage)
- 1x [[Diver's Armor|Diver's Shirt]] (Baar) or 1x [[Diver's Armor|Diver's Trunks]] (Alchemage)
- 32x [[Enchanted Gold Block]]
- 5x [[Golden Powder]]
- 32x [[Enchanted Golden Carrot]]
- 1x [[Golden Ball]]
Which costs roughly from the [[Bazaar]], alongside a [[Snorkeling Armor|Snorkeling Visor]] and [[Snorkeling Armor|Snorkeling Pants]].
## Usage
Wand:

## Obtaining
### Hunting
A Thunder Shard can be obtained from killing a [[Thunder]]. Since it is an [[Elusive]] mob, it is guaranteed to be [[Charm Chance|Charmed]] and drop one.
### Fusion
A Thunder Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|ID Fusion]] and [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]].

## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Thunder Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Thunder Speed]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Thunder Speed ([[Attributes]]); Your [[Empty Thunder Bottle]], [[Empty Storm Bottle]], and [[Empty Hurricane Bottle]] fill +1%–10% faster.
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].

Raw Materials (All materials in their most basic form needed to obtain the item.): 153,600x (2,400 stacks (153,600 items total)) [[Lapis Lazuli]] 3x [[Sand]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: TITANIC_EXP_BOTTLE
Tooltip: Smash it open to receive experience!; RARE
A Titanic Experience Bottle is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Consumables|consumable]]. It is unlocked at [[Lapis Lazuli|Lapis Lazuli VIII (8)]].
A Titanic Experience Bottle grants a base amount of 250,000 [[Experience]] at [[Enchanting|Enchanting 0]], up to 1,000,000 [[Experience]] at [[Enchanting|Enchanting LX (60)]] without other buffs.
## Obtaining
A Titanic Experience Bottle can be crafted, fished up, looted from [[Ender Nodes]], or obtained from the [[Experimentation Table]].
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Lapis Lazuli VIII (8) ([[Lapis Lazuli]])|1x [[Glass Bottle]] 6x [[Enchanted Lapis Lazuli Block]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Lapis Lazuli Block]] ×6 + [[Glass Bottle]] → Titanic Experience Bottle|
### [[Fishing]]
Titanic Experience Bottle is a very rare drop from [[Fishing]] (0.1% chance). It grants +300 [[Fishing|Fishing XP]] and +1 [[Enchanting|Enchanting XP]].
### [[Ender Nodes]]
A Titanic Experience Bottle has a 1.31% (1 in 76.39) chance to drop from mining an Obsidian [[Ender Nodes|Ender Node]].
### [[Experimentation Table]]
A Titanic Experience Bottle can be obtained from the higher tiers of Superpairs. They are relatively common.
### [[Mage Emissary]]
Titanic Experience Bottles can be bought from the [[Mage Emissary]] in [[Scarleton]] for 750,000 [[Coins]] if the player is part of the [[Factions|Mage Faction]]. A discount of up to 20% is provided depending on the player's [[Factions|ቾ Mage Reputation]].
### Raffle of the Century
Titanic Experience Bottle can also be obtained as a MINOR reward from [[Raffle of the Century]], or from the [[Gift Bag of the Century]].

# Titanium
Aliases: Titanium Ore
Categories: Rare items, Items in Sacks, Items, Confirmations needed, Crafting ingredients, Dwarven Mines, Mining, Ores
Its strength and resistance to corrosion are the highest of any metallic element.
[Titanium]
Type: Item
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
Tooltip Text: Its strength and resistance to corrosion are the highest of any metallic element.
[Material Tiers]
← Previous: None
Next →: [[Enchanted Titanium]]
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No

Sacks: Small Mining Sack ([[Mining Sack]]); Medium Mining Sack ([[Mining Sack]]); Large Mining Sack ([[Mining Sack]])
[Shop]
Sell: 20 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: TITANIUM_ORE
Tooltip: Its strength and resistance to corrosion are the highest of any metallic element.; RARE DWARVEN METAL
Titanium can appear when mining [[Mithril]] in the [[Dwarven Mines]]. It can spawn from mining any block that drops [[Mithril]]. The block does not turn back to Mithril until it has been mined by a player. To be mined having at least 5 [[Breaking Power]] is required.
## Obtaining
### Mining
Titanium can only be mined by a pickaxe with at least 5 [[Breaking Power]].
- It takes the appearance of a Polished Diorite block once it spawns.
- It has a 0.5% base chance to replace a [[Mithril]] block when mined in the [[Dwarven Mines]]. The spawn rate can be increased:
  - to 2% by unlocking the Titanium Insanium perk in the [[Heart of the Mountain]] menu, and up to 7% at Level 50 (7.1% with [[Blue Cheese Goblin Omelette]]).
  - to an additional 6% during [[Mining Fiesta|Mining Fiestas]] by unlocking the Titanium Tenacity perk from [[Doug]].
The drop amount is affected by [[Mining Fortune]] with the base amount being [[Titanium]] 2 Titaniums.

## Usage
With 4 [[Enchanted Titanium]] and a [[Bejeweled Handle]], players can purchase a [[Titanium Pickaxe]] from [[Bubu]]'s shop in the [[Dwarven Village]].
At [[The Forge]], 16 of its [[Enchanted Titanium|enchanted form]] can be refined to make [[Refined Titanium]].
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Enchanted Titanium]]|None|[[Titanium]] 160x (2 stacks plus 32 (160 in total)) Titanium|Recipe: Titanium x32 ×7 → [[Enchanted Titanium]]|
## Commissions
When the player is introduced to [[Commissions]], they get a quest of mining 2 Titanium anywhere.
After that, the [[King]] can give Commissions that involve mining Titanium. The following quests involving Titanium may appear in the Commission #1 and #3 slot:
- Mine 10 Titanium in a specific [[Dwarven Mines]] area.
- Mine 15 Titanium anywhere.
## Tips
- There is a chance for the Sky Mall perk to grant 5x multiplier to all obtained Titanium for 1 SkyBlock day, this can be extremely profitable with right setup.
## Trivia
- Using the [[Compact]] [[Enchantments|Enchantment]] or [[Reforging|Refined]] [[Reforging|Reforge]], players have a chance to gain [[Enchanted Titanium]] when mining Titanium ore.
- Before [[Compact]] was reworked, players could exploit it, causing a crash in the price of Titanium. This has been patched.
- Titanium is unable to be insta-mined.

The Titanium Belt can be forged in [[The Forge]] once the player has reached [[Heart of the Mountain|Heart of the Mountain III (3)]]. It takes 4 hours and 30 minutes to forge.

Titanium Equipment is an [[Equipment]] Set obtained through various [[The Forge|Forging]]. Each piece provides a 10 [[Mining Fortune]] bonus when mining [[Mithril]] and [[Titanium]]. The Full Set Bonus: True Dwarf grants additional 50 [[Mining Speed]] and 40 [[Mining Fortune]] while in the [[Dwarven Mines]]. This set is the upgraded form of the [[Mithril Equipment]] set.
## Set

- [[Titanium Cloak]]
- [[Titanium Belt]]
- [[Titanium Gauntlet]]

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: TITANIUM_LANTERN
Tooltip: Mining Deployable; Ability: Deploy RIGHT CLICK Place an orb for 5m buffing up to 5; players within 30 blocks.; Costs 50% of max mana.; Orb Buff: Titanium Lantern ▪ Grants +60 Mining Speed.; ▪ Grants +15 Mining Fortune.; ▪ Grants +5 Heat Resistance.; Only one deployable buff applies.; ❣ Requires Heart of the Mountain Tier 5.; RARE DEPLOYABLE
A Titanium Lantern is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Deployables|Deployable]].
## Obtaining
### Forge
A Titanium Lantern can be forged once the player has reached [[Heart of the Mountain|Heart of the Mountain V (5)]]. It takes 4 hours to forge.
### Forging Tree
- [[Titanium Lantern]] 1 Titanium Lantern
  - 1 [[Mithril Lantern]] 1 [[Dwarven Lantern]] 1 [[Refined Mithril]] 160 (2 stacks plus 32) [[Enchanted Mithril]] 25,600 (400 stacks) [[Mithril]] 16 [[Enchanted Emerald]] 2,560 (40 stacks) [[Emerald]]
  - 2 [[Refined Titanium]] 32 [[Enchanted Titanium]] 5,120 (80 stacks) [[Titanium]]
  - 1 [[Enchanted Emerald Block]] 160 [[Enchanted Emerald]] 25,600 [[Emerald]]
## Usage
Using the ability of a Titanium Lantern costs 50% of player's max [[Mana]], this can be reduced down to 40% using [[Mana Disintegrator|Mana Disintegrators]]. Casting the ability grants up to 5 players in 30 block radius the following stats:
- +60 [[Mining Speed]]
- +15 [[Mining Fortune]]
- +5 [[Heat Resistance]]
When used alongside multiple or different lantern deployables, only one deployable effect applies and only the one of higher rarity will apply its effects.

Tooltip: Consumed on use; Pet Items can boost pets in many powerful ways! A pet can only hold one Pet Item, but you can swap it at any time!; The pet must be visible to apply the item!; Grants +33.3 Mining Fortune while mining Titanium Ore.; Right-click on your summoned pet to give it this item!; RARE PET ITEM
Titanium Minecart is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Pet Items|Pet Item]].
## Obtaining
Titanium Minecart has a 20% chance per roll to be obtained from participating in active mining events in the [[Dwarven Mines]]. Up to two items may roll per event.
## Usage
Right-clicking a [[Pets|Pet]] whilst holding a Titanium Minecart will equip it to the [[Pets|Pet]], granting it +33.3 [[Mining Fortune]] while mining [[Titanium|Titanium Ore]]. If another [[Pet Items|Pet Item]] were equipped on the [[Pets|Pet]], it would refund the Titanium Minecart for 100,000 [[Coins]] and remove the perks it gave.

## Obtaining
The Titanium Ring can be forged once the player has reached [[Heart of the Mountain|Heart of the Mountain III (3)]]. It requires 6 [[Refined Titanium]], 1 [[Titanium Talisman]], and 20 hours to forge.
### Forging Tree
- [[Titanium Ring]] 1 Titanium Ring
  - 6 [[Refined Titanium]] 96 (1 stack plus 32) [[Enchanted Titanium]] 15,360 (240 stacks) [[Titanium]]
  - 1 [[Titanium Talisman]] 2 [[Refined Titanium]] 32 [[Enchanted Titanium]] 5,120 (80 stacks) [[Titanium]]
## Usage
### Forging
The Titanium Ring, and 12 [[Refined Titanium]] can be used to forge a [[Titanium Artifact]] once the player has reached [[Heart of the Mountain|Heart of the Mountain IV (4)]]. It takes 36 hours to forge.
#### Forging Tree
- 1 [[Titanium Artifact]]
  - 12 [[Refined Titanium]] 192 (3 stacks) [[Enchanted Titanium]] 30,720 (480 stacks) [[Titanium]]

- Upgrading this accessory will remove all [[Enrichments]] and [[Recombobulator 3000|Recombobulator 3000s]] when it is used in the Forge to obtain the next version of the accessory.
- The [[Titanium Talisman]], Titanium Ring, [[Titanium Artifact]] and [[Titanium Relic]] used to stack for a total of 150 [[Mining Speed]].

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: TITANIUM_TESSERACT
Tooltip: Reforge Stone Combinable in Anvil; Applies the Dimensional reforge when combined with Armor.; Grants the following stats that scale with the item's rarity:;  Mining Speed ☯ Mining Wisdom; Dimensional Bonus Titanium drops +10 ᠅ Mithril Powder when mined.; Right-click to view rarity scaling!; ❣ Requires Mining Skill 20.; RARE REFORGE STONE
Titanium Tesseract is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Reforge Stones|Reforge Stone]] that applies the [[Reforging|Dimensional]] [[Reforging|Reforge]] to [[Armor]]. It grants [[Mining Speed]] and [[Mining Wisdom]].
## Obtaining
The Titanium Tesseract can be forged at [[The Forge]] after reaching [[Heart of the Mountain]] Tier III. It requires 1 [[Refined Titanium]] and 16 [[Enchanted Lapis Lazuli]] and takes 6 hours to forge.
### Forging Tree
- [[Titanium Tesseract]] 1 Titanium Tesseract
  - 1 [[Refined Titanium]] 16 [[Enchanted Titanium]] 2,560 (40 stacks) [[Titanium]]
  - 16 [[Enchanted Lapis Lazuli]] 2,560 [[Lapis Lazuli]]
## Usage
### Reforging
Titanium Tesseract can be applied to [[Armor]] using an [[Anvil]] or [[The Hex]] to apply the [[Reforging|Dimensional]] reforge, to grant [[Mining Speed]] and [[Mining Wisdom]], which scale with the armor's rarity. This has a skill requirement of [[Mining|Mining XX (20)]] and can be done with any armor, regardless of the piece. Armor that is already reforged to [[Reforging|Dimensional]] will grant the stats, even if the player doesn't meet the requirement to apply it.
Additionally, it grants the Dimensional Bonus, which grants +10 ᠅ [[Mithril Powder]] when [[Titanium|Titanium Ore]] is mined.

The Titanium-Infused Fuel Tank is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Drill Parts|Drill Part]] that can be obtained from forging in [[The Forge]] in the [[Dwarven Mines]]. It increases the [[Machine Fuel|Fuel]] capacity of [[Drills]] to 25,000 and reduces the cooldowns of [[Heart of the Mountain|Pickaxe Abilities]] by 4%.
## Obtaining
The Titanium-Infused Fuel Tank can be forged once the player has reached [[Heart of the Mountain|Heart of the Mountain V (5)]]. It takes 30s to forge.
### Forging Tree
- [[Titanium-Infused Fuel Tank]] 1 Titanium-Infused Fuel Tank
  - 1 [[Mithril-Infused Fuel Tank]] 5 [[Refined Diamond]] 10 [[Enchanted Diamond Block]] 1,600 (25 stacks) [[Enchanted Diamond]] 256,000 (4,000 stacks) [[Diamond]] 10 [[Refined Mithril]] 1,600 [[Enchanted Mithril]] 256,000 [[Mithril]] 5 [[Fuel Canister]] 10 [[Enchanted Coal Block]] 1,600 [[Enchanted Coal]] 256,000 [[Coal]]
  - 10 [[Refined Titanium]] 160 (2 stacks plus 32) [[Enchanted Titanium]] 25,600 (400 stacks) [[Titanium]]
  - 5 [[Refined Diamond]] 10 [[Enchanted Diamond Block]] 1,600 [[Enchanted Diamond]] 256,000 [[Diamond]]
  - 5 [[Fuel Canister]] 10 [[Enchanted Coal Block]] 1,600 [[Enchanted Coal]] 256,000 [[Coal]]
## Usage
The Titanium-Infused Fuel Tank can be applied to any [[Drills|Drill]] via [[Drill Anvil]], to set its fuel capacity to 25,000 and reduce the cooldowns of [[Heart of the Mountain|Pickaxe Abilities]] by 4%. It can then be removed via the same menu for 9,999 [[Coins]], which gives the item back to the player.
### Forging
It is also used in forging the [[Gemstone Fuel Tank]], which requires [[Heart of the Mountain|Heart of the Mountain VII (7)]] and takes 30 seconds.

[Item Metadata]

Tooltip: Drill Part; +150 Mining Speed +15 Mining Fortune; Put this item in the Drill Engine slot of a Drill at a Drill Mechanic!; ❣ Requires Heart of the Mountain Tier 5.; RARE
Titanium-Plated Drill Engine is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Drill Parts|Drill Part]] that can be obtained from forging in [[The Forge]] in the [[Dwarven Mines]]. It increases [[Drills]]' [[Mining Speed]] by +150 and [[Mining Fortune]] by +15.
## Obtaining
Titanium-Plated Drill Engine can be forged once the player has reached [[Heart of the Mountain|Heart of the Mountain V (5)]]. It takes 30 seconds to forge.
### Forging Tree
- [[Titanium-Plated Drill Engine]] 1 Titanium-Plated Drill Engine
  - 1 [[Mithril-Plated Drill Engine]] 2 [[Drill Motor]] 20 [[Treasurite]] 2 [[Enchanted Iron Block]] 320 (5 stacks) [[Enchanted Iron Ingot]] 51,200 (800 stacks) [[Iron Ingot]] 6 [[Enchanted Redstone Block]] 960 (15 stacks) [[Enchanted Redstone Dust]] 153,600 (2,400 stacks) [[Redstone Dust]] 2 [[Golden Plate]] 2 [[Refined Diamond]] 4 [[Enchanted Diamond Block]] 640 (10 stacks) [[Enchanted Diamond]] 102,400 (1,600 stacks) [[Diamond]] 4 [[Enchanted Gold Block]] 640 [[Enchanted Gold Ingot]] 102,400 [[Gold Ingot]] 10 [[Glacite Jewel]] 1 [[Mithril Plate]] 1 [[Refined Titanium]] 16 [[Enchanted Titanium]] 2,560 (40 stacks) [[Titanium]] 5 [[Refined Mithril]] 800 (12 stacks plus 32) [[Enchanted Mithril]] 128,000 (2,000 stacks) [[Mithril]] 1 [[Golden Plate]] 1 [[Refined Diamond]] 2 [[Enchanted Diamond Block]] 320 [[Enchanted Diamond]] 51,200 [[Diamond]] 2 [[Enchanted Gold Block]] 320 [[Enchanted Gold Ingot]] 51,200 [[Gold Ingot]] 5 [[Glacite Jewel]] 1 [[Enchanted Iron Block]] 160 (2 stacks plus 32) [[Enchanted Iron Ingot]] 25,600 (400 stacks) [[Iron Ingot]]
  - 8 [[Refined Titanium]] 128 (2 stacks) [[Enchanted Titanium]] 20,480 (320 stacks) [[Titanium]]
  - 2 [[Drill Motor]] 20 [[Treasurite]] 2 [[Enchanted Iron Block]] 320 [[Enchanted Iron Ingot]] 51,200 [[Iron Ingot]] 6 [[Enchanted Redstone Block]] 960 [[Enchanted Redstone Dust]] 153,600 [[Redstone Dust]] 2 [[Golden Plate]] 2 [[Refined Diamond]] 4 [[Enchanted Diamond Block]] 640 [[Enchanted Diamond]] 102,400 [[Diamond]] 4 [[Enchanted Gold Block]] 640 [[Enchanted Gold Ingot]] 102,400 [[Gold Ingot]] 10 [[Glacite Jewel]]
## Usage
The Titanium-Plated Drill Engine can be applied to any [[Drills|Drill]] via [[Drill Anvil]], to increase its stats by +150 [[Mining Speed]] and +15 [[Mining Fortune]]. It can then be removed via the same menu for 9,999 [[Coins]], which gives the item back to the player.
### Forging
It is also used in forging the [[Ruby-Polished Drill Engine]], which requires [[Heart of the Mountain|Heart of the Mountain VII (7)]] and takes 30 seconds.

# Tool Exp Capsule
Aliases: Tool EXP Capsule
Categories: Rare items, Items
[Tool Exp Capsule]
Type: Item
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Sell: 100,000 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: TOOL_EXP_CAPSULE
Tooltip: Combinable in Anvil; Grants +100,000 Exp to any of your Farming Tools.; RARE
A Tool Exp Capsule is a [[Rarity|RARE]] item.
## Obtaining
A Tool Exp Capsule can be obtained from a max level [[Farming Tools|Farming Tool]]. Upon reaching level 50, all experience gained goes towards a Tool Exp Capsule. After reaching 200,000 experience, the progress resets and the player is given a Tool Exp Capsule, and the following message will appear:
OVERFLOW! Your <tool> has just dropped a Tool Exp Capsule!
## Usage
A Tool Exp Capsule can be applied to any specialized Farming Tool to gain 100,000 tool XP.
## Trivia
- To get a tool from level 0 to 50 with just Tool Exp Capsules, it would take 494 capsules (costing from the [[Bazaar]]).

Fishing Rod:

# Travel Scroll to Critter Safari Entrance
Categories: Rare items, Items, Travel Scrolls
[Travel Scroll to Critter Safari Entrance]
Type: Travel Scroll ([[Travel Scrolls]])
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]

Tooltip: Consume this item to add its destination to your Fast Travel options.; Island: Torrhus Canyon Teleport: Critter Safari Entrance; RARE TRAVEL SCROLL
The Travel Scroll to Critter Safari Entrance is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Travel Scrolls|Travel Scroll]] that unlocks [[Fast Travel]] to the [[Critter Safari Entrance]].
## Obtaining
### Chapters
The Travel Scroll to Critter Safari Entrance can be obtained once as a reward from completing the [[Torrhus Canyon]]'s third [[Chapters|Chapter]]. It can then be purchased repeatedly from [[Rusty]] for 50,000 [[Coins]].
## Usage
### Fast Travel
The Travel Scroll to Critter Safari Entrance can be consumed to unlock the [[Fast Travel]] option to the [[Critter Safari Entrance]]. This includes access to /warp safari, /warp crittersafari, and /warp critter_safari.
### Portalizer
The Travel Scroll to Critter Safari Entrance can be combined with a [[Portalizer]] in an [[Anvil]] to create the [[Portals|Portal to the Critter Safari Entrance]].

Tooltip: Consume this item to add its destination to your Fast Travel options.; Island: Deep Caverns Teleport: Spawn; RARE TRAVEL SCROLL
The Travel Scroll to Deep Caverns is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Travel Scrolls|Travel Scroll]] that unlocks [[Fast Travel]] to the [[Deep Caverns]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Redstone V (5) ([[Redstone Dust]])|16x [[Enchanted Ender Pearl]] 48x [[Enchanted Redstone Dust]] 80x (1 stack plus 16 (80 in total)) [[Enchanted Lapis Lazuli]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Redstone Dust]] x16 ×3 + [[Enchanted Lapis Lazuli]] x16 ×5 + [[Enchanted Ender Pearl]] x16 → Travel Scroll to Deep Caverns|

### Fast Travel
The Travel Scroll to Deep Caverns can be consumed to unlock the [[Fast Travel]] option to the [[Deep Caverns]]. This includes access to /warp deep.
### Portalizer
The Travel Scroll to Deep Caverns can be combined with a [[Portalizer]] in an [[Anvil]] to create the [[Portals|Portal to the Deep Caverns]]. This portal can also be crafted.

## Obtaining
### The Forge
The Travel Scroll to the Dwarven Base Camp can be forged once the player has reached [[Heart of the Mountain|Heart of the Mountain VII (7)]]. It takes 10 hours to forge.
#### Forging Tree
- [[Travel Scroll to the Dwarven Base Camp]] 1 Travel Scroll to the Dwarven Base Camp
  - 1 [[Gemstone|Flawless Onyx Gemstone]] 80 (1 stack plus 16) [[Gemstone|Fine Onyx Gemstone]] 6,400 (100 stacks) [[Gemstone|Flawed Onyx Gemstone]] 512,000 (8,000 stacks) [[Gemstone|Rough Onyx Gemstone]]
  - 16 [[Enchanted Ender Pearl]] 320 (5 stacks) [[Ender Pearl]]
  - 500,000 (7,812 stacks plus 32) [[Coins]]
## Usage
### Fast Travel
The Travel Scroll to the Dwarven Base Camp can be consumed to unlock the [[Fast Travel]] option to [[Dwarven Base Camp]] in the [[Dwarven Mines]]. This includes access to /warp basecamp, /warp base, /warp camp, /warp glacite, /warp tunnel, /warp tunnels, and /warp gt.
### Portalizer
The Travel Scroll to the Dwarven Base Camp can be combined with a [[Portalizer]] in an [[Anvil]] to create the [[Portals|Portal to the Dwarven Base Camp]].

# Treasure Bait
Categories: Rare items, Items in Sacks, Items, Fishing
[Treasure Bait]
Type: [[Bait]]
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Sacks: Small Bait Sack ([[Bait Sack]]); Medium Bait Sack ([[Bait Sack]]); Large Bait Sack ([[Bait Sack]])
[Shop]
Sell: 500 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: TREASURE_BAIT
Tooltip: Fishing Bait Consumes on Cast; Grants +10 Fishing Speed and +2 Treasure Chance.; RARE BAIT
Treasure Bait is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Bait]] that grants [[Treasure Chance]].
## Obtaining
### Purchasing
[[Treasure Bait]] 16 Treasure Bait can be purchased from [[Junker Joel]] for 1 [[Rusty Coin]].
### Seal Pet
The [[Rarity|RARE]], [[Rarity|EPIC]], and [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Seal Pet]]'s Peak Performance ability materializes [[Treasure Bait]] Treasure Bait in the player's inventory upon catching [[Treasure]].
## Usage
### Fishing
Treasure Bait can be kept in either the player's inventory or [[Bait Sack]]. When casting a [[Fishing Rods|Fishing Rod]], a Treasure Bait will be consumed to grant +10 [[Fishing Speed]] and +2 [[Treasure Chance]]. Note that the [[Fishing Speed]] and [[Treasure Chance]] don't show up in the player's stats. The effects last until the player reels in their rod. If the player doesn't catch anything when reeling, or otherwise cancels their catch, the Bait will be refunded.
## History

|Mar 18, 2025|0.22|Added Treasure Bait.|
|Jul 8, 2026|0.26|Added a custom texture for Treasure Bait as part of the SkyBlock Resource Pack.|

## Obtaining
### Dungeon Secrets
A Treasure Talisman is obtainable from [[Dungeon Secrets]] in [[The Catacombs - Floor IV]] and above.
### Raffle of the Century
Treasure Talisman can also be obtained as a MINOR reward from [[Raffle of the Century]], or from the [[Gift Bag of the Century]].

A Treasure Talisman can be salvaged for 15 [[Gold Essence]].

- A Treasure Talisman upgraded by a [[Recombobulator 3000]] keeps its upgrade when crafted into a [[Treasure Ring]] when placed at the top-left position in the crafting recipe.

The Tribal Spear is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Swords|Sword]].
## Obtaining
The Tribal Spear can be purchased from [[Odawa]] for 1 [[Jungle Heart]] and 100 (1 stack plus 36) [[Sludge Juice]].
## Usage
Sword:
Tribal Spear [[Strength]]: +100
[Effects]
[Ability: Thwack ON RIGHT CLICK; Throw the spear like an arrow which will return to you.]
The Thwack Ability pierces through infinite[Confirm (Confirmation needed; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)] mobs, traveling 20 blocks.
## Trivia
- While on cooldown, the Tribal Spear's texture turns into a [[Ghast Tear]].
- This is the only item with a Right Click Ability being able to be used as a guest on someone's [[Private Island]].
  - However, they cannot damage mobs.
- This is a very cheap and efficient way to stunlock [[Thorn]] in [[The Catacombs - Floor IV]]. If Thorn gets un-stunlocked, he will move faster, making him almost impossible to hit.
- Multiple Spears can be thrown at once.
## Gallery
- In-hand model.

Tooltip: Essence of Fossils I Gain +1% more Fossil Essence.; You can Syphon this shard from your Hunting Box.; Right-click to send to Hunting Box!; Shift Right-click to move all!; ❣ Requires Hunting Skill 10.; RARE WATER SHARD (ID R86)
The Troodon Shard is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Essence of Fossils]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Hunting
A Troodon Shard can be obtained from [[Hunting]] a [[Troodon]] with a [[Critter Capsule]] in the [[Critter Safari]].
### Fusion
A Troodon Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]].
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Troodon Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Essence of Fossils]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Essence of Fossils ([[Attributes]]); Gain +1%–+10% more [[Fossil Essence]].
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].
|Level|Shards; Required|Total; Shards|Total; SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|Ⅰ (1)|1|1|+1 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅱ (2)|2|3|+2 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅲ (3)|3|6|+3 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅳ (4)|3|9|+4 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅴ (5)|4|13|+5 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅵ (6)|4|17|+6 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅶ (7)|5|22|+7 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅷ (8)|6|28|+8 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅸ (9)|8|36|+9 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅹ (10)|12|48|+10 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
### Fusion
A Troodon Shard can be used in [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]] to create other [[Attributes|Shards]].

## Lore
When viewed in the [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] or [[Bazaar]], the Troodon Shard has the following description:
- Seems to have been frozen for a prolonged period of time.

Tungsten Plate is a [[Rarity|RARE]] item that can be obtained from forging in [[The Forge]] in the [[Dwarven Mines]].
## Obtaining
Tungsten Plate can be forged once the player has reached [[Heart of the Mountain|Heart of the Mountain VII (7)]] and [[Tungsten|Tungsten VI (6)]]. It requires 4 [[Refined Tungsten]], 1 [[Glacite Amalgamation]] and 3 hours to forge.
### Forging Tree
- [[Tungsten Plate]] 1 Tungsten Plate
  - 4 [[Refined Tungsten]] 640 (10 stacks) [[Enchanted Tungsten]] 102,400 (1,600 stacks) [[Tungsten]]
  - 1 [[Glacite Amalgamation]] 4 [[Gemstone|Fine Onyx Gemstone]] 320 (5 stacks) [[Gemstone|Flawed Onyx Gemstone]] 25,600 (400 stacks) [[Gemstone|Rough Onyx Gemstone]] 4 [[Gemstone|Fine Aquamarine Gemstone]] 320 [[Gemstone|Flawed Aquamarine Gemstone]] 25,600 [[Gemstone|Rough Aquamarine Gemstone]] 4 [[Gemstone|Fine Citrine Gemstone]] 320 [[Gemstone|Flawed Citrine Gemstone]] 25,600 [[Gemstone|Rough Citrine Gemstone]] 4 [[Gemstone|Fine Peridot Gemstone]] 320 [[Gemstone|Flawed Peridot Gemstone]] 25,600 [[Gemstone|Rough Peridot Gemstone]] 256 (4 stacks) [[Enchanted Glacite]] 40,960 (640 stacks) [[Glacite]]
### Frozen Corpses
Tungsten Plates can be obtained from opening [[Frozen Corpses]].
|Type|Quantity|Chance Per Roll|
|-|-|-|
|Tungsten Tungsten|[[Tungsten Plate]] 1 Tungsten Plate|0.08% (1 in 1308)|
|Vanguard|[[Tungsten Plate]] 1 Tungsten Plate|0.85% (1 in 117.17)|
