[Item Metadata]
Item ID: TUNGSTEN_KEYCHAIN
Tooltip: Drill Part; Part Type: Upgrade Module; Every 100 fuel consumed increases your  Heat or decreases your  Cold by 1.; Can be applied to Drills by talking to a Drill Mechanic!; RARE
A Tungsten Regulator is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Drill Parts|Drill Part]].
## Obtaining
A Tungsten Regulator can be forged at [[The Forge]] upon reaching [[Heart of the Mountain|Heart of the Mountain VIII (8)]]. It can be forged with 1 [[Gemstone|Perfect Opal Gemstone]], 5 [[Drills|Fuel Tank]] and 5 [[Tungsten Plate]]. It takes 13 hours and 30 minutes to forge.
### Forging Tree
- [[Tungsten Regulator]] 1 Tungsten Regulator
  - 1 [[Gemstone|Perfect Opal Gemstone]] 5 [[Gemstone|Flawless Opal Gemstone]] 400 (6 stacks plus 16) [[Gemstone|Fine Opal Gemstone]] 32,000 (500 stacks) [[Gemstone|Flawed Opal Gemstone]] 2,560,000 (40,000 stacks) [[Gemstone|Rough Opal Gemstone]] 1 [[Gemstone Crystals|Opal Crystal]]
  - 5 [[Fuel Canister]] 10 [[Enchanted Coal Block]] 1,600 (25 stacks) [[Enchanted Coal]] 256,000 (4,000 stacks) [[Coal]]
  - 5 [[Tungsten Plate]] 20 [[Refined Tungsten]] 3,200 (50 stacks) [[Enchanted Tungsten]] 512,000 (8,000 stacks) [[Tungsten]] 5 [[Glacite Amalgamation]] 20 [[Gemstone|Fine Onyx Gemstone]] 1,600 [[Gemstone|Flawed Onyx Gemstone]] 128,000 (2,000 stacks) [[Gemstone|Rough Onyx Gemstone]] 20 [[Gemstone|Fine Aquamarine Gemstone]] 1,600 [[Gemstone|Flawed Aquamarine Gemstone]] 128,000 [[Gemstone|Rough Aquamarine Gemstone]] 20 [[Gemstone|Fine Citrine Gemstone]] 1,600 [[Gemstone|Flawed Citrine Gemstone]] 128,000 [[Gemstone|Rough Citrine Gemstone]] 20 [[Gemstone|Fine Peridot Gemstone]] 1,600 [[Gemstone|Flawed Peridot Gemstone]] 128,000 [[Gemstone|Rough Peridot Gemstone]] 1,280 (20 stacks) [[Enchanted Glacite]] 204,800 (3,200 stacks) [[Glacite]]
## Usage
The Tungsten Regulator can be applied to any [[Drills|Drill]] via [[Drill Anvil]]. When applied, every 100 [[Machine Fuel|Fuel]] consumed decreases [[Heat]] or [[Cold]] by 1. It can then be removed via the same menu for 9,999 [[Coins]], which gives the item back to the player.

# Tuning Fork
Categories: Rare items, Items
[Tuning Fork]
Type: Item
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
Source: [[Personal Harp]]
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Sell: 100 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: TUNING_FORK
Tooltip: Left-Click: Pick tune!; Right-Click note block: Tune it!; Tuning: [pitch]; Earned from perfecting Pachelbel on the personal harp.; RARE
The Tuning Fork is a [[Rarity|RARE]] item obtained by getting a 100% score on "Pachelbel", the final prodigy song found on the [[Personal Harp]]. Every time you complete "Pachelbel" with a perfect score, you get another Tuning Fork.
## Item Abilities
Left clicking the Tuning Fork will open a GUI where the player can pick what pitch they wish the noteblock to be. Once they have picked, the sound is played, and the item's lore shows the current pitch. Right clicking with the item will set the pitch of the targeted noteblock to that pitch.
## Trivia
- Upon obtaining the Tuning Fork, the player is greeted with the message "Earned %%Item%% from perfecting this song!"
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Apr 21, 2021|0.11.4|Added Tuning Fork.|
Linked from: [[Personal Harp]]

- The Turbomax Vacuum Cleaner cannot contain more than 8 vermins total. The player must empty the Vacuum Cleaner in the [[Kat (Rift)|Vermin Bin]] before vacuuming more insects.

# Turtlellini
Aliases: Turtellini
Categories: Rare items, Items in Sacks, Items, Mutations
[Turtlellini]
Type: Mutation
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✕ No
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Sacks: Small Mutations Sack ([[Mutations Sack]]); Medium Mutations Sack ([[Mutations Sack]]); Large Mutations Sack ([[Mutations Sack]])
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: TURTLELLINI
Tooltip: Outside, it's smooth, but there's more to it than meets the eye.; Analyze this Mutation at the Crop Analyzer in Jake's Underground Lab.; EPIC MUTATION
A Turtlellini is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Mutations|Mutation]].
## Obtaining
### Greenhouse
A Turtlellini can grow in the [[Greenhouse]] if there are 4 [[Soggybud]] and 4 [[Choconut]] within 1 block of farmland.
When placed near another crop, it retains water by 50% and grants immunity to negative effects.
## Crop Drops
When harvested, Turtlellinis also drop 800 (12 stacks plus 32) [[Melon Slice]] and +800 [[Farming|Farming XP]].
## Usage
### Crop Analyzer
A Turtlellini can be analyzed in the [[Crop Analyzer]] for 240,000 [[Coins]]. When analyzed, it grants 120 [[Copper]]. The first time analyzing a Turtlellini grants an additional 750 [[Copper]].
### Mutations
A Turtlellini can be turned into [[Shellfruit]] with [[Blastberry]].
## Gallery
- Turtlellini in the Greenhouse Turtlellini in the [[Greenhouse]]
- Cracked Turtlellini after using a Blastberry Cracked Turtlellini after using a [[Blastberry]]

The Twilight Dagger is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Swords|Sword]]. It is unlocked at [[Blaze Slayer|Blaze Slayer II (2)]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Blaze Slayer II (2) ([[Blaze Slayer]])|1x [[Molten Powder]] 1x [[Stick]] 5x [[Enchanted Sulphur]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Sulphur]] x5 + [[Molten Powder]] + [[Stick]] → Twilight Dagger|
## Usage
Sword:
Twilight Dagger [[Damage]]: +90 [[Strength]]: +45 [[Crit Damage]]: +15
[Effects]
[Deal 1.5x damage to Infernal (Infernal (Mob Type)) mobs.; Deal 1.2x damage to Skeletal mobs.; Gain +15 Combat Wisdom against Infernal (Infernal (Mob Type)) mobs. Ability: Attunement ON RIGHT CLICK; Swap between Spirit and Crystal mode in order to attack through Hellion Shields. Ability: Wraith Drain; On hit, heal for 200 + 4% Health after 6s if the Hellion Shield has switched attunement.]
The Twilight Dagger deals 1.5x damage to [[Infernal (Mob Type)|Infernal]] mobs and 1.2x damage to [[Skeletal]] mobs. Additionally, it provides +15 [[Combat Wisdom]] against [[Infernal (Mob Type)|Infernal]] mobs.
The Twilight Dagger has 2 abilities:
- Attunement allows the wielder to swap between Spirit and Crystal attunements in order to attack through the [[Inferno Demonlord]]'s Hellion Shield ability. When switching attunement, the Twilight Dagger changes appearance from a Iron Sword to a Golden Sword.
- Wraith Drain allows the wielder to heal 200 [[Health]] + 4% after 6s upon hit if the Hellion Shield has switched attunement.
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Mawdredge Dagger]]|Blaze Slayer IV (4) ([[Blaze Slayer]])|1x [[Enchanted Sulphur Cube]] [[Twilight Dagger]] 1x Twilight Dagger 8x [[Molten Powder]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Sulphur Cube]] + [[Molten Powder]] x8 + Twilight Dagger → [[Mawdredge Dagger]]|

An Umber Plate is a [[Rarity|RARE]] item that can be obtained from forging in [[The Forge]] in the [[Dwarven Mines]].
## Obtaining
An Umber Plate can be forged once the player has reached [[Heart of the Mountain|Heart of the Mountain VII (7)]] and [[Umber|Umber VI (6)]]. It requires 4 [[Refined Umber]], 1 [[Glacite Amalgamation]] and 3 hours to forge.
### Forging Tree
- [[Umber Plate]] 1 Umber Plate
  - 4 [[Refined Umber]] 640 (10 stacks) [[Enchanted Umber]] 102,400 (1,600 stacks) [[Umber]]
  - 1 [[Glacite Amalgamation]] 4 [[Gemstone|Fine Onyx Gemstone]] 320 (5 stacks) [[Gemstone|Flawed Onyx Gemstone]] 25,600 (400 stacks) [[Gemstone|Rough Onyx Gemstone]] 4 [[Gemstone|Fine Aquamarine Gemstone]] 320 [[Gemstone|Flawed Aquamarine Gemstone]] 25,600 [[Gemstone|Rough Aquamarine Gemstone]] 4 [[Gemstone|Fine Citrine Gemstone]] 320 [[Gemstone|Flawed Citrine Gemstone]] 25,600 [[Gemstone|Rough Citrine Gemstone]] 4 [[Gemstone|Fine Peridot Gemstone]] 320 [[Gemstone|Flawed Peridot Gemstone]] 25,600 [[Gemstone|Rough Peridot Gemstone]] 256 (4 stacks) [[Enchanted Glacite]] 40,960 (640 stacks) [[Glacite]]
### Frozen Corpses
Umber Plates can be obtained from opening [[Frozen Corpses]].

|-|-|-|
|Umber Tungsten|[[Umber Plate]] 1 Umber Plate|0.08% (1 in 1308)|
|Vanguard|[[Umber Plate]] 1 Umber Plate|0.85% (1 in 117.17)|

Tooltip: Ability: Deploy RIGHT CLICK Place an orb for 5m buffing up to 5; players within 30 blocks.; Mana Cost: 50% of max; Orb Buff: Umberella Grants +5 Trophy Chance, doubled during Weather!; Only one deployable buff applies.; RARE DEPLOYABLE
An Umberella is an [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Deployables|Deployable]] unlocked at [[Umber|Umber IX (9)]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Umber IX (9) ([[Umber]])|2x [[Stick]] 6x [[Refined Umber]]|Recipe: [[Refined Umber]] x2 ×3 + [[Stick]] ×2 → Umberella|
## Usage
Using the ability of an Umberella costs 50% of player's max [[Mana]]. This can be reduced to 40% with [[Mana Disintegrator|Mana Disintegrators]]. Placing an Umberella grants up to 5 players in a 30 block radius 5 [[Trophy Chance]]. During [[Weather]], the stats double, becoming 10 [[Trophy Chance]].

## Obtaining
### Mob Drops

- Tier III: 2.01%
- Tier IV: 0.6%

## Obtaining
An Unripe Jerryseed can be obtained by talking to [[Xalx]] in the [[Goblin Holdout]] while an [[Unfulfilled Jerryseed]] and [[Mysterious Crop]] are in the player's inventory.
## Usage
Talking to [[Dirt Guy]] with an Unripe Jerryseed [[Reforging|Reforged]] to [[Reforging|Dirty]] to gives the player a [[Fertilized Jerryseed]].
## Trivia
- Despite being considered a [[Swords|sword]], this item does not provide any combat stats.
  - Furthermore, both [[Fertilized Jerryseed]] and [[Unfulfilled Jerryseed]] are not considered swords, despite being directly related.

[Materials]
Raw Materials (All materials in their most basic form needed to obtain the item.): 40,960x (640 stacks (40,960 items total)) [[Diamond]] 40,960x [[Rotten Flesh]] 40,752x (636 stacks plus 48 (40,752 in total)) [[Pumpkin]] 320x (5 stacks (320 items total)) [[Pufferfish]] 256x (4 stacks (256 items total)) [[Flint Arrow]] 160x (2 stacks plus 32 (160 in total)) [[Ghast Tear]] 2x [[Planks]] 2x [[Stick]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: VOODOO_DOLL
Tooltip: Ability: Acupuncture RIGHT CLICK Shoot arrows from every direction around the targeted monster.; Monsters hit by at least one arrow are slowed and receive 1,500 damage\ s for 10s.; Mana Cost: 200 Cooldown: 5s; Who do you voodoo?; ☠ Requires Zombie Slayer 5.; RARE
The Voodoo Doll is a [[Rarity|RARE]] item. It is unlocked at [[Zombie Slayer|Zombie Slayer V (5)]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Zombie Slayer V (5) ([[Zombie Slayer]])|1x [[Crystallized Heart]] 1x Pumpkin Minion IX ([[Pumpkin Minion]]) 2x [[Enchanted Pufferfish]] 32x [[Enchanted Ghast Tear]] 256x [[Flint Arrow]]|Recipe: [[Flint Arrow]] x64 ×4 + [[Enchanted Ghast Tear]] x32 + [[Enchanted Pufferfish]] ×2 + [[Pumpkin Minion]] + [[Crystallized Heart]] → Voodoo Doll|
## Usage
Item:
Voodoo Doll
[Effects]
[Ability: Acupuncture RIGHT CLICK; Shoot arrows from every direction around the targeted monster.; Monsters hit by at least one arrow are slowed and receive 1,500 damage/s for 10s.; Mana Cost (Intelligence): 200; Cooldown: 5; Intelligence Scaling (Damage Calculation): 1]
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Jinxed Voodoo Doll]]|Wilted Berberis III (3) ([[Wilted Berberis]])|[[Voodoo Doll]] 1x Voodoo Doll 1x Voidling Minion II ([[Voidling Minion]]) 1x [[Zombie's Heart]] 3x [[Enchanted Pufferfish]] 13x [[Enchanted Ghast Tear]] 64x [[Wilted Berberis Bunch]]|Recipe: [[Wilted Berberis Bunch]] x16 ×4 + [[Enchanted Pufferfish]] x3 + [[Voidling Minion]] + [[Enchanted Ghast Tear]] x13 + [[Zombie's Heart]] + Voodoo Doll → [[Jinxed Voodoo Doll]]|
The Voodoo Doll's Acupuncture ability can be activated by aiming at any entity within a range of 18 blocks. Activating the ability summons a volley of 9 arrows around the targeted mobs: 4 in the cardinal horizontal directions, 4 aimed 45˚ downwards in the cardinal diagonal directions, and 1 above the mob that travels downward.
All mobs that are hit by the volley of arrows take their base damage, as well as an additional 1,500 base damage as damage per second over 10s. The ability costs 200 [[Mana]] to use and has a 5s cooldown.
## Trivia
- Attempting to feed the [[Siamese Lynxes|Siamese Lynx]] a Voodoo Doll will result in the message This mob doesn't eat voodoo. Previously, the item would be consumed.
- The Voodoo Doll could not be used against players in the [[Colosseum]] prior to its removal.
- The Voodoo Doll can target mobs summoned as the player: this includes but is not limited to Bats spawned from the [[Spirit Sceptre]], Withers spawned from the Witherborn full set bonus, minions summoned from the Ragnarok ability, and mobs summoned with [[Necromancy]].
- The Voodoo Doll's description "Who do you voodoo?" is a reference to the video game Dead Island, where at the start of the game, the song "Who Do You Voodoo" will play.
- The cooldown on both voodoo dolls is shared, for example if a player uses a Voodoo Doll, they will have a 5 second cooldown on any Jinxed Voodoo Doll and vice versa.

|Jul 1, 2020|0.7.11|Fixed magic damage scaling.|
|Jul 13, 2021|The crafting recipe now uses Flint Arrows ([[Flint Arrow]]).|
|Jul 12, 2023|0.19.1|Fixed color issue in the Voodoo Doll lore.|

The Vulture Shard is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Helix Sharpening]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Shop Purchase
A Vulture Shard can be purchased from [[Miria]] for 25 [[Miria's Coupon]] after reaching [[Miria|Miria Milestone IV]].
### Fusion
A Vulture Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]].
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Vulture Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Helix Sharpening]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Helix Sharpening ([[Attributes]]); Grants +15–150 [[Sweep]] towards Helix Trees ([[Helix Log]]).
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].
|Level|Shards; Required|Total; Shards|Total; SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|Ⅰ (1)|1|1|+1 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅱ (2)|2|3|+2 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅲ (3)|3|6|+3 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅳ (4)|3|9|+4 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅴ (5)|4|13|+5 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅵ (6)|4|17|+6 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅶ (7)|5|22|+7 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅷ (8)|6|28|+8 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅸ (9)|8|36|+9 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅹ (10)|12|48|+10 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
### Fusion
A Vulture Shard can be used in [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]] to create other [[Attributes|Shards]].

## Lore
When viewed in the [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] or [[Bazaar]], the Vulture Shard has the following description:
- Their bald heads and powerful beaks are perfectly adapted for their scavenging lifestyle, allowing them to feed efficiently on carcasses.

The Wand of Farming is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Wands|Wand]] used to farm crops in the [[Rift Dimension|Rift]].
## Obtaining
The Wand of Farming is obtained after talking to [[Shania (Rift)|Shania]] in the [[Dreadfarm]].
## Usage
The Wand of Farming can be used to farm [[Agaricus Cap|Agaricus Caps]], [[Caducous Stem|Caducous Stems]], and [[Wilted Berberis]] in [[Dreadfarm]], as well as [[Riftwart Roots]] in [[West Village]].

Tooltip: Ability: Medium Heal RIGHT CLICK Heal 80❤ per second for 5s.; Wand heals don't stack.; Vitality Cost: 25 Cooldown: 1s; ☠ Requires Zombie Slayer 3.; RARE WAND
The Wand of Mending is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Wands|Healing Wand]] and [[Support Items|Support Item]] unlocked at [[Zombie Slayer|Zombie Slayer III (3)]]. It is an upgraded version of the [[Wand of Healing]] and can be upgraded into the [[Wand of Restoration]]. It can only be crafted and used after reaching [[Zombie Slayer|Zombie Slayer III (3)]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Zombie Slayer III (3) ([[Zombie Slayer]])|1x [[Wand of Healing]] 1x [[Revenant Viscera]] 12x [[Enchanted Dark Oak Log]]|Recipe: [[Revenant Viscera]] + [[Wand of Healing]] + [[Enchanted Dark Oak Log]] x12 → Wand of Mending|
## Usage
Wand:
Wand of Mending
[Effects]
[Ability: Medium Heal RIGHT CLICK; Heal 80 Health/s for 5s.; Wand heals don't stack.; Vitality Cost (Vitality): 25; Cooldown: 1s]
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Wand of Restoration]]|Zombie Slayer VI (6) ([[Zombie Slayer]])|[[Wand of Mending]] 1x Wand of Mending 16x [[Revenant Viscera]] 64x [[Enchanted Dark Oak Log]]|Recipe: [[Revenant Viscera]] x16 + Wand of Mending + [[Enchanted Dark Oak Log]] x64 → [[Wand of Restoration]]|
## Tips
- The set bonuses of [[Revenant Armor]] and [[Reaper Armor]] increase healing from [[Wands|Healing Wands]] by 50%.
- Each armor piece enchanted with [[Habanero Tactics|Habanero Tactics V]] grants 7.5% additional healing from [[Wands|Healing Wands]].
## Trivia
- Although consecutive casts do not stack, the first healing tick is applied immediately. With enough [[Vitality]], repeatedly casting the ability can heal 160 [[Health]] per second.

[SkyBlock Prototype]

The Warding Diamathame is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Wands (disambiguation)|Wand]] in the [[Rift Dimension]].
## Obtaining
A Warding Diamathame can be claimed from [[Sorcerer Okron]] at the beginning of their quest. A Warding Diamathame can then be purchased from them for 1,000 [[Motes]] after imbuing the Glyphs on all sides of Okron's house.

[Whale Bait]
Type: [[Bait]]
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]

[Shop]

[Materials]

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: WHALE_BAIT
Tooltip: Fishing Bait Consumes on Cast; Grants +30 Fishing Speed and increases your chance to catch rare Sea Creatures.; RARE BAIT
Whale Bait is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Bait]] that grants [[Fishing Speed]] and boosts the player's chance to catch rare [[Sea Creatures]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Lily Pad VI (6) ([[Lily Pad]])|1x [[Dark Bait]] 1x [[Light Bait]] 1x [[Fish Bait]]|Recipe: [[Fish Bait]] + [[Light Bait]] + [[Dark Bait]] → Whale Bait|
|Lily Pad VI (6) ([[Lily Pad]])|1x [[Raw Salmon]] 1x [[Ink Sac]] 2x [[Prismarine Crystals]] 4x [[Raw Cod]]|Recipe: [[Raw Cod]] x4 + [[Ink Sac]] + [[Raw Salmon]] + [[Prismarine Crystals]] x2 → Whale Bait|
### Seal's Treat Bag
128x Whale Bait have a 5.5% (1 in 18) chance to be obtained from a [[Seal's Treat Bag]].
## Usage
### Fishing
Whale Bait can be kept in either the player's inventory or [[Bait Sack]]. When casting a [[Fishing Rods|Fishing Rod]], a Whale Bait will be consumed to grant +30 [[Fishing Speed]] and increase the weight of rare [[Sea Creatures]] (those with a weight below 400) by 1.25x. Note that the [[Fishing Speed]] doesn't show up in the player's stats. The effects last until the player reels in their rod. This effect is multiplicative with [[Tracking]]. If the player doesn't catch anything when reeling, or otherwise cancels their catch, the Bait will be refunded.

Tooltip: Can be consumed inside a Kuudra chest to reroll the Shard inside into a higher rarity one.; May the eternal luck of Kuudra be in your favor.; RARE
Wheel of Fate is a [[Rarity|RARE]] item obtainable from [[Kuudra]] that allows the [[Player]] to reroll the [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]] inside a [[Kuudra|Kuudra Chest]].
## Obtaining
Wheel of Fate has a chance to appear in [[Kuudra|Paid Kuudra Chests]].
Drop Chances:
- Basic: 1.35%
- Hot: 1.35%
- Burning: 1.33%
- Fiery: 1.33%
- Infernal: 1.23%
## Usage
Wheel of Fate can be used on a Kuudra Chest that has an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], similar to a [[Kismet Feather]]. Upon using, the Wheel of Fate will be consumed and the Chest's [[Attributes]] will have been rerolled.[Confirm (Confirmation needed; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)]

The Wiki Tiki Shard is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Construct Fortune]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Hunting
A Wiki Tiki Shard can be obtained from killing a [[Wiki Tiki]]. Since it is an [[Elusive]] mob, it is guaranteed to be [[Charm Chance|Charmed]] and drop one.
### Fusion
A Wiki Tiki Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]] and [[Attribute Fusion|Special Fusion]]. For [[Attribute Fusion|Special Fusion]], the following input conditions must be met:
- First: [[Rarity|EPIC]] or higher and Creation Family
- Second: [[Sneaky Tiki Shard]], [[Shrieky Tiki Shard]], or [[Cheeky Tiki Shard]]
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Wiki Tiki Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Construct Fortune]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Construct Fortune ([[Attributes]]); Grants +0.5–5 [[Magic Find]] on [[Construct]] mobs.
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].
|Level|Shards; Required|Total; Shards|Total; SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|Ⅰ (1)|1|1|+1 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅱ (2)|2|3|+2 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅲ (3)|3|6|+3 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅳ (4)|3|9|+4 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅴ (5)|4|13|+5 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅵ (6)|4|17|+6 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅶ (7)|5|22|+7 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅷ (8)|6|28|+8 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅸ (9)|8|36|+9 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅹ (10)|12|48|+10 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
### Fusion
A Wiki Tiki Shard can be used in [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]] to create other [[Attributes|Shards]].

## Lore
When viewed in the [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] or [[Bazaar]], the Wiki Tiki Shard has the following description:
- It remains unclear as to how the Wiki Tiki gained sentience, or who made it that way.
